    82	/// Rewrite only plain-key column tokens. Index labels, refusal codes/messages
    83	/// and every other literal retain their current authored spelling.
    84	pub(super) fn plain_declaration(file: &str, raw: &str, index: &ast::IndexDecl, prior: &ast::TableDecl, next: &ast::TableDecl) -> String {
    85	    if !index.is_plain() { return raw.into(); }
    86	    let Some(columns) = column_names(prior, next) else { return raw.into() };
    87	    let (tokens, diagnostics) = lexer::lex(file, raw);
    88	    if !diagnostics.is_empty() { return raw.into(); }
    89	    let Some(keyword) = tokens.iter().position(|token| matches!(token.value.as_str(), "by" | "unique")) else { return raw.into() };
    90	    let mut cursor = keyword + 1;
    91	    if tokens.get(cursor + 1).is_some_and(|token| token.value == ":") { cursor += 2; }
    92	    let mut edits = Vec::new();
    93	    for (ordinal, column) in index.columns.iter().enumerate() {
    94	        let Some(token) = tokens.get(cursor).filter(|token| &token.value == column) else { return raw.into() };
    95	        if let Some(old) = columns.get(column) {
    96	            let start = raw.split_inclusive('\n').take(token.site.line.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(str::len).sum::<usize>();
    97	            let start = start + raw[start..].chars().take(token.site.col.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(char::len_utf8).sum::<usize>();
    98	            edits.push((start, start + token.value.len(), old.clone()));
    99	        }
   100	        cursor += 1;
   101	        if ordinal + 1 < index.columns.len() {
   102	            if !tokens.get(cursor).is_some_and(|token| token.value == ",") { return raw.into(); }
   103	            cursor += 1;
   104	        }
   105	    }
   106	    let mut source = raw.to_owned();
   107	    edits.sort_by_key(|edit| std::cmp::Reverse(edit.0));
   108	    for (start, end, value) in edits { source.replace_range(start..end, &value); }
   109	    source
   110	}
   111	
   112	/// Retain every named dependency, without introducing an unused duplicate
   113	/// unique key that changes an otherwise unambiguous positional point lookup.
   114	pub(super) fn prune_unused_equivalent_keys(sources: &mut [(String, String)], current: &[(String, String)], changes: &mut Vec<String>) {
   115	    let mut docs=sources.iter().map(|(f,t)|crate::parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   116	    let current=current.iter().map(|(f,t)|crate::parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   117	    let mut used=BTreeSet::new();
   118	    let mut visit=|value:&mut ast::Expr|match value {
   119	        ast::Expr::Read(read)|ast::Expr::Exists{read,..}|ast::Expr::Created{read,..}=>{
   120	            if let Some(index)=&read.index {used.insert((read.table.clone(),index.clone()));}
   121	        }
   122	        ast::Expr::Merge(merge)=>{used.insert((merge.table.clone(),merge.index.clone()));}
   123	        _=>{}
   124	    };
   125	    for doc in &mut docs {
   126	        crate::bind::intent_walk::document(doc,&mut visit);
   127	        for table in &mut doc.tables {
   128	            for rule in &mut table.rules {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut rule.value,&mut visit);}
   129	            for value in table.column_read_values_mut() {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   130	            for column in &mut table.columns {
   131	                if let Some(value)=&mut column.default {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   132	                if let Some(value)=column.maintained.as_mut().and_then(|m|m.filter.as_mut()) {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   133	            }
   134	            for(value,_)in &mut table.final_exists {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   135	            for index in &mut table.indexes {for value in index.computed.values_mut(){crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}}
   136	        }
   137	        for invariant in &mut doc.invariants {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut invariant.value,&mut visit);}
   138	        for maintain in &mut doc.maintains {
   139	            for value in &mut maintain.keys {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   140	            if let Some(value)=&mut maintain.filter {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   141	            if let Some(rule)=&mut maintain.read {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut rule.value,&mut visit);}
   142	        }
   143	        for timeline in &mut doc.timelines {if let Some(value)=&mut timeline.filter {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}}
   144	        for view in &mut doc.views {for branch in &mut view.branches {
   145	            if let Some(value)=&mut branch.predicate {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   146	            for field in &mut branch.fields {if let ast::ShapeField::Field{value,..}=field{crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}}
   147	        }}
   148	        for view in &mut doc.feed_views {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut view.predicate,&mut visit);}
   149	        for action in &mut doc.actions {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut action.rule,&mut visit);}
   150	        for arg in doc.programs.iter_mut().flat_map(|p|&mut p.args)
   151	            .chain(doc.functions.iter_mut().flat_map(|f|&mut f.args))
   152	            .chain(doc.actions.iter_mut().flat_map(|a|&mut a.args)) {
   153	            if let Some(value)=&mut arg.default {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value,&mut visit);}
   154	        }
   155	    }
   156	    for doc in &docs {for table in &doc.tables {
   157	        if let Some((_,ast::SyncOrder::Index(index)))=table.sync.as_ref().and_then(|s|s.horizon.as_ref()) {
   158	            used.insert((table.name.clone(),index.clone()));
   159	        }
   160	    }}
   161	    for ((_,text),doc) in sources.iter_mut().zip(&docs) {
   162	        let mut edits=Vec::new();
   163	        for table in &doc.tables {
   164	            let mut groups=BTreeMap::<Vec<String>,Vec<&ast::IndexDecl>>::new();
   165	            for index in table.indexes.iter().filter(|i|i.unique&&i.is_plain()) {groups.entry(index.columns.clone()).or_default().push(index);}
   166	            for group in groups.values().filter(|g|g.len()>1) {
   167	                let named=group.iter().filter(|i|used.contains(&(table.name.clone(),i.name.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   168	                let preferred=current.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables)
   169	                    .filter(|t|t.name==table.name||t.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&table.name)||table.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&t.name))
   170	                    .flat_map(|t|&t.indexes).find_map(|i|group.iter().find(|old|old.name==i.name));
   171	                let keep=preferred.unwrap_or(&group[0]);
   172	                for index in group {
   173	                    if used.contains(&(table.name.clone(),index.name.clone())) || (named.is_empty()&&index.name==keep.name) {continue}
   174	                    let start=text.split_inclusive('\n').take(index.site.line.saturating_sub(1)as usize).map(str::len).sum::<usize>();
   175	                    let end=text[start..].find('\n').map_or(text.len(),|n|start+n+1);
   176	                    edits.push((start,end));
   177	                    changes.push(format!("{}: omitted unused equivalent unique index {}; the identical ordered key remains enforced",table.name,index.name));
   178	                }
   179	            }
   180	        }
   181	        edits.sort_by_key(|edit|std::cmp::Reverse(edit.0));
   182	        for(start,end)in edits {text.replace_range(start..end,"");}
   183	    }
   184	}

     1	//! Compare aligned policy meaning without confusing a key with its label.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — compiled key meaning and every guard operand survive a visible rollback.
     3	use snapback4_core::{Schema, schema::{Constraint, Horizon, RuleExpr, Sync}};
     4	
     5	pub struct Policies<'a> {
     6	    view: &'a Schema,
     7	    current: &'a Schema,
     8	}
     9	
    10	impl<'a> Policies<'a> {
    11	    pub fn new(view: &'a Schema, current: &'a Schema) -> Self { Self { view, current } }
    12	
    13	    fn index(&self, table: &str, view: &str, current: &str) -> bool {
    14	        match (self.view.table(table).and_then(|t|t.indexes.get(view)),
    15	            self.current.table(table).and_then(|t|t.indexes.get(current))) {
    16	            (Some(view),Some(current))=>view==current,
    17	            _=>false,
    18	        }
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    pub fn constraint(&self, view: &Constraint, current: &Constraint) -> bool {
    22	        match (view,current) {
    23	            (Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table:a,index:ai,key:ak},
    24	                Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table:b,index:bi,key:bk})=>
    25	                a==b&&ak==bk&&self.index(a,ai,bi),
    26	            _=>view==current,
    27	        }
    28	    }
    29	
    30	    pub fn rule(&self, view: &RuleExpr, current: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
    31	        use RuleExpr::*;
    32	        match (view,current) {
    33	            (Exists{table:a,index:ai,key:ak,provenance:ap,equalities:ae},
    34	                Exists{table:b,index:bi,key:bk,provenance:bp,equalities:be}) |
    35	            (Created{table:a,index:ai,key:ak,provenance:ap,equalities:ae},
    36	                Created{table:b,index:bi,key:bk,provenance:bp,equalities:be})=>
    37	                a==b&&ak==bk&&ap==bp&&ae==be&&self.index(a,ai,bi),
    38	            (And(a),And(b))|(Or(a),Or(b))=>a.len()==b.len()&&a.iter().zip(b).all(|(a,b)|self.rule(a,b)),
    39	            (Not(a),Not(b))=>self.rule(a,b),
    40	            _=>view==current,
    41	        }
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    pub fn optional_rule(&self, view: Option<&RuleExpr>, current: Option<&RuleExpr>) -> bool {
    45	        match (view,current) { (Some(a),Some(b))=>self.rule(a,b),(None,None)=>true,_=>false }
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    fn horizon(&self, table: &str, view: Option<&Horizon>, current: Option<&Horizon>) -> bool {
    49	        match (view,current) {
    50	            (Some(a),Some(b))=>a.last==b.last&&self.index(table,&a.by,&b.by),
    51	            (None,None)=>true,
    52	            _=>false,
    53	        }
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    pub fn sync(&self, table: &str, view: Option<&Sync>, current: Option<&Sync>) -> bool {
    57	        match (view,current) {
    58	            (Some(a),Some(b))=>a.audience==b.audience&&self.horizon(table,a.horizon.as_ref(),b.horizon.as_ref())
    59	                &&a.also.len()==b.also.len()&&a.also.iter().zip(&b.also).all(|(a,b)|
    60	                    a.audience==b.audience&&self.horizon(table,a.horizon.as_ref(),b.horizon.as_ref())),
    61	            (None,None)=>true,
    62	            _=>false,
    63	        }
    64	    }
    65	}
    66	
    67	#[cfg(test)]
    68	mod tests {
    69	    use super::*;
    70	    use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::KeyProvenance, schema::{Audience, RuleTerm}};
    71	    fn schema(name: &str, key: &str) -> Schema {
    72	        let source=format!("table target:\n  code: text <=20\n  other: text <=20\n  unique {name}: {key}\n  public\n  online only\n");
    73	        let bound=crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source)]);
    74	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",bound.diagnostics);bound.schema
    75	    }
    76	    fn probe(name: &str) -> RuleExpr {
    77	        RuleExpr::Exists{table:"target".into(),index:name.into(),key:vec![RuleTerm::Field("value".into())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Shape,equalities:Vec::new()}
    78	    }
    79	    #[test]
    80	    fn equivalent_labels_keep_all_probe_operands_and_provenance() {
    81	        let view=schema("oldKey","code");let current=schema("newKey","code");let policy=Policies::new(&view,&current);
    82	        let a=probe("oldKey");let b=probe("newKey");assert!(policy.rule(&a,&b));
    83	        let mut changed=b.clone();if let RuleExpr::Exists{key,..}=&mut changed{*key=vec![RuleTerm::Field("different".into())];}assert!(!policy.rule(&a,&changed));
    84	        let mut changed=b.clone();if let RuleExpr::Exists{provenance,..}=&mut changed{*provenance=KeyProvenance::Const;}assert!(!policy.rule(&a,&changed));
    85	        let mut changed=b.clone();if let RuleExpr::Exists{equalities,..}=&mut changed{equalities.push(("code".into(),RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String("a".into()))));}assert!(!policy.rule(&a,&changed));
    86	        let created=match b.clone(){RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,provenance,equalities}=>RuleExpr::Created{table,index,key,provenance,equalities},_=>unreachable!()};assert!(!policy.rule(&a,&created));
    87	        let ca=Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table:"target".into(),index:"oldKey".into(),key:vec![RuleTerm::Field("value".into())]};
    88	        let cb=Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table:"target".into(),index:"newKey".into(),key:vec![RuleTerm::Field("value".into())]};assert!(policy.constraint(&ca,&cb));
    89	        assert!(policy.rule(&RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(a.clone())),&RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(b.clone()))));
    90	        assert!(!policy.rule(&RuleExpr::And(vec![a]),&RuleExpr::Or(vec![b])));
    91	    }
    92	    #[test]
    93	    fn a_shared_label_cannot_hide_a_different_key_or_uniqueness() {
    94	        let view=schema("key","code");let mut current=schema("key","other");
    95	        assert!(!Policies::new(&view,&current).rule(&probe("key"),&probe("key")));
    96	        current=schema("key","code");current.tables.get_mut("target").unwrap().indexes.get_mut("key").unwrap().unique=false;
    97	        assert!(!Policies::new(&view,&current).rule(&probe("key"),&probe("key")));
    98	        assert!(!Policies::new(&view,&current).rule(&probe("missing"),&probe("missing")));
    99	    }
   100	    #[test]
   101	    fn a_window_keeps_its_audience_limit_and_complete_key() {
   102	        let view=schema("oldKey","code");let current=schema("newKey","code");let policy=Policies::new(&view,&current);
   103	        let a=Sync::single(Audience::Public,Some(Horizon{last:10,by:"oldKey".into()}));
   104	        let mut b=Sync::single(Audience::Public,Some(Horizon{last:10,by:"newKey".into()}));
   105	        assert!(policy.sync("target",Some(&a),Some(&b)));
   106	        b.horizon.as_mut().unwrap().last=20;assert!(!policy.sync("target",Some(&a),Some(&b)));
   107	        b.horizon.as_mut().unwrap().last=10;b.audience=Audience::Column("code".into());assert!(!policy.sync("target",Some(&a),Some(&b)));
   108	        assert!(!policy.sync("target",Some(&a),None));
   109	    }

   264	pub(super) struct Installed {
   265	    dir: PathBuf,
   266	    parent: u64,
   267	    ids: Vec<u64>,
   268	    pub pending: bool,
   269	}
   270	impl Drop for Installed {
   271	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   272	        if !self.pending && super::releases::selectable(&self.dir, self.parent).is_err() {
   273	            for id in &self.ids {
   274	                let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(self.dir.join("releases").join(id.to_string()));
   275	            }
   276	        }
   277	    }
   278	}
   279	pub(super) fn install(
   280	    dir: &Path,
   281	    stage: &Path,
   282	    parent: u64,
   283	    views: &[View],
   284	) -> Result<Installed, String> {
   285	    let mut installed = Installed {
   286	        dir: dir.into(),
   287	        parent,
   288	        ids: Vec::new(),
   289	        pending: false,
   290	    };
   291	    for v in views {
   292	        let from = stage.join("rollback-views").join(v.release.to_string());
   293	        crate::release_files::durable(&from)?;
   294	        fs::rename(&from, dir.join("releases").join(v.release.to_string()))
   295	            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   296	        installed.ids.push(v.release);
   297	    }
   298	    if stage.join("rollback-views").exists() {
   299	        fs::remove_dir(stage.join("rollback-views")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   300	    }
   301	    Ok(installed)
   302	}
   303	
   304	pub(super) fn needs_registration(dir:&Path,matched:Option<u64>,prepared:&[View])->Result<bool,String>{
   305	    let held=matched.map(|n|manifest(&dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))).transpose()?.unwrap_or_default();
   306	    for view in &held{verify(dir,view)?;}
   307	    Ok(held.len()!=prepared.len()||prepared.iter().any(|v|!held.iter().any(|old|old.target==v.target&&old.digest==v.digest&&old.hides==v.hides)))
   308	}

     1	//! A rollback view joins value domains and retains current authored authority.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — generated source is reviewable; no automatic business-policy synthesis.
     3	use crate::{ast::{self,TypeKind}, lexer::{self,Kind}, parser, print};
     4	use std::collections::BTreeMap;
     5	pub struct Bridge {pub sources:Vec<(String,String)>,pub changes:Vec<String>,pub diagnostics:Vec<crate::diag::Diagnostic>}
     6	
     7	/// Injective current-to-view column identities from authored rename declarations.
     8	/// Shared by index source transport and CLI hard-constraint admission.
     9	pub use super::release_bridge_indexes::column_names as declared_column_names;
    10	pub use super::release_bridge_indexes::align_current_schema;
    11	pub use super::release_bridge_policy::Policies as PolicyComparison;
    12	
    13	/// Source-domain inclusion includes text minima/formats, which live outside
    14	/// ColumnType in the compiled schema. Never infer admission from advice prose.
    15	pub fn requires_view(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    16	    // An executable repair behind unchanged data and intent must not restore
    17	    // the old implementation. Ordinary schema/intent evolution is governed by
    18	    // visibility facts below, rather than the mere presence of an invariant.
    19	    if same_data_and_intent(target,current)
    20	        && !crate::compile(current).invariants.is_empty()
    21	        && modules(target)!=modules(current) {return true;}
    22	    let docs=current.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    23	    let tables=docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    24	    let mut aliases=tables.iter().filter_map(|t|t.renamed_from.as_ref().map(|old|(t.name.clone(),old.clone()))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    25	    for(file,text)in target{for t in parser::parse_document(file,text).0.tables{if let Some(prior)=t.renamed_from{aliases.insert(t.name,prior);}}}
    26	    fn normalize(t:&mut ast::ScalarType,aliases:&BTreeMap<String,String>){match &mut t.kind{TypeKind::Ref{table}=>{let mut seen=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();while let Some(old)=aliases.get(table){if !seen.insert(table.clone()){*table=seen.into_iter().min().unwrap();break}*table=old.clone();}},TypeKind::List{item,..}=>normalize(item,aliases),_=>()}}
    27	    if super::release_bridge_constraints::relaxed(target,current) {return true;}
    28	    for(file,text)in target {
    29	        for old in parser::parse_document(file,text).0.tables {
    30	            let Some(next)=tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==old.name||t.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&old.name)||old.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&t.name))else{continue};
    31	            for col in &old.columns {
    32	                let Some(candidate)=next.columns.iter().find(|c|c.name==col.name||c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&col.name)||col.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&c.name))else{continue};
    33	                let scalar=!matches!(&col.type_.kind,TypeKind::Ref{..}|TypeKind::List{..}|TypeKind::Image{..}|TypeKind::File{..}|TypeKind::Video{..});
    34	                if scalar&&docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.downfills).any(|d|d.table==next.name&&matches!(&d.value,ast::Expr::Object{fields,..}if fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==col.name))){continue;}
    35	                let mut from=candidate.type_.clone();let mut to=col.type_.clone();normalize(&mut from,&aliases);normalize(&mut to,&aliases);
    36	                if !crate::client_contracts::accepts(&crate::types::scalar_to_arg(&from),&crate::types::scalar_to_arg(&to)){return true}
    37	            }
    38	            let Some(columns)=declared_column_names(&old,next)else{return true};
    39	            let normalize_columns=|fields:&[String]|fields.iter().map(|c|columns.get(c).unwrap_or(c).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    40	            if old.indexes.iter().any(|i|i.unique&&!next.indexes.iter().any(|n|n.unique&&normalize_columns(&n.columns)==i.columns)){return true}
    41	            if old.caps.iter().any(|c|!next.caps.iter().any(|n|normalize_columns(&n.by)==c.by&&n.max<=c.max)){return true}
    42	        }
    43	    }false
    44	}

    46	fn modules(sources:&[(String,String)])->Vec<(String,String)> {
    47	    let mut out=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|(std::path::Path::new(file).file_stem().unwrap_or(std::ffi::OsStr::new("schema")).to_string_lossy().into_owned(),text.clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();out.sort();out
    48	}

    50	pub(super) fn operation_name(file:&str,name:&str)->String {
    51	    let module=std::path::Path::new(file).file_stem().and_then(|s|s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
    52	    if module=="schema"{name.to_owned()}else{format!("{module}.{name}")}
    53	}

    55	/// Compare authored data/intent declarations and executable signatures, while
    56	/// excluding executable bodies. Module identity and wire signatures still count.
    57	pub fn same_data_and_intent(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    58	    fn signature(sources:&[(String,String)])->Vec<(String,Vec<(lexer::Kind,String)>)> {
    59	        modules(sources).into_iter().map(|(module,text)| {
    60	            let doc=parser::parse_document(&module,&text).0;
    61	            let mut edits=Vec::new();
    62	            for site in doc.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.site).chain(doc.functions.iter().map(|p|&p.site)) {
    63	                let start=offset(&text,site.line,1);
    64	                let declaration=declaration(&text,site.line);
    65	                let (tokens,_)=lexer::lex(&module,&declaration);
    66	                let mut depth=0;
    67	                if let Some(colon)=tokens.iter().find(|token| {
    68	                    match token.value.as_str(){"("|"["|"{"=>depth+=1,")"|"]"|"}"=>depth-=1,_=>()}
    69	                    token.value==":"&&depth==0
    70	                }) {
    71	                    let end=(start+declaration.len()).min(text.len());
    72	                    let body=start+offset(&declaration,colon.site.line,colon.site.col)+1;
    73	                    edits.push((body,end));
    74	                }
    75	            }
    76	            let mut text=text;
    77	            edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));
    78	            for (start,end) in edits {text.replace_range(start..end,"\n");}
    79	            let (tokens,_)=lexer::lex(&module,&text);
    80	            (module,tokens.into_iter().map(|t|(t.kind,t.value)).collect())
    81	        }).collect()
    82	    }
    83	    signature(target)==signature(current)
    84	}
    85	
    86	pub fn loosen(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->Bridge {
    87	    if !crate::compile(current).invariants.is_empty() {
    88	        let mut allow_guarded=false;
    89	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut allow_guarded);
    90	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty() || !allow_guarded{return complete;}
    91	        // A complete current closure is preferred, but a selective guarded
    92	        // predecessor is also truthful when its retained contracts compile.
    93	        let guarded=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
    94	        if guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){return guarded;}
    95	        return Bridge{diagnostics:complete.diagnostics.into_iter().chain(guarded.diagnostics).collect(),..guarded};
    96	    }
    97	    let guarded=loosen_guarded(target,current);
    98	    if !guarded.diagnostics.is_empty(){
    99	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut false);
   100	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty(){return complete;}
   101	        let current_bridge=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
   102	        if current_bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(){return current_bridge;}
   103	    }
   104	    guarded
   105	}
   106	
   107	fn loosen_guarded(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->Bridge {
   108	    let new: BTreeMap<_,_>=current.iter().flat_map(|(file,text)|parser::parse_document(file,text).0.tables.into_iter().map(move|t|(t.name.clone(),(file,text,t)))).collect();
   109	    let mut new=new;
   110	    for (_,(_,_,table)) in new.clone() {if let Some(old)=&table.renamed_from {if let Some(value)=new.get(&table.name).cloned(){new.insert(old.clone(),value);}}}
   111	    for(file,text)in target {for old in parser::parse_document(file,text).0.tables {if let Some(prior)=&old.renamed_from {if !new.contains_key(&old.name){if let Some(value)=new.get(prior).cloned(){new.insert(old.name.clone(),value);}}}}}
   112	    let mut domain_diagnostics=Vec::new();let mut changes=Vec::new();let mut affected=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();let mut sources=target.to_vec();let mut names=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
   113	    for (file,text) in &mut sources {
   114	        let document=parser::parse_document(file,text).0;let mut edits=Vec::new();
   115	        for table in &document.tables {
   116	            names.insert(table.name.clone());
   117	            let Some((_,_,next))=new.get(&table.name) else {continue};
   118	            if next.name!=table.name {affected.insert(table.name.clone());changes.push(format!("table {}: current spelling {}; target spelling routes the same identity",table.name,next.name));}
   119	            for column in &table.columns {
   120	                let Some(candidate)=next.columns.iter().find(|c|c.name==column.name || c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&column.name)||column.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&c.name)) else {continue};
   121	                if candidate.name!=column.name {affected.insert(table.name.clone());changes.push(format!("{}.{}: current column spelling {}; target spelling routes the same cell",table.name,column.name,candidate.name));}
   122	                let Some(joined)=join(&column.type_,&candidate.type_,&new) else {domain_diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&column.site,format!("rollback view for {}.{} has incompatible predecessor/current domains {} / {}",table.name,column.name,print::scalar_type(&column.type_),print::scalar_type(&candidate.type_)),"author a truthful compatible source and typed migration before deploying").guide("deploy"));continue};
   123	                // A current guard may rely on a value being impossible under its
   124	                // schema. Never reintroduce predecessor-only values behind that
   125	                // same predicate merely because another column needs widening.
   126	                let mut current_domain=join(&candidate.type_,&candidate.type_,&new).unwrap();
   127	                let mut joined_domain=joined.clone();
   128	                fn normalize(t:&mut ast::ScalarType,tables:&BTreeMap<String,(&String,&String,ast::TableDecl)>){
   129	                    match &mut t.kind {
   130	                        TypeKind::Ref{table}=>{if let Some((_,_,next))=tables.get(table){*table=next.name.clone();}},
   131	                        TypeKind::List{item,..}=>normalize(item,tables),
   132	                        TypeKind::Enum{variants}=>variants.sort(), _=>()
   133	                    }
   134	                }
   135	                normalize(&mut current_domain,&new);normalize(&mut joined_domain,&new);
   136	                if current_domain!=joined_domain {
   137	                    domain_diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&column.site,format!("rollback view for {}.{} would admit predecessor values excluded by the current domain; current authority may depend on their absence",table.name,column.name),"author a compatible rollback source preserving the current domain and truthful migrations; do not widen a current confidentiality or safety boundary").guide("deploy"));
   138	                }
   139	                if joined==column.type_ {continue;}
   140	                affected.insert(table.name.clone());
   141	                if let Some((start,end))=type_span(file,text,column) {
   142	                    edits.push((start,end,print::scalar_type(&joined)));
   143	                    changes.push(format!("{}.{}: {} → {}",table.name,column.name,print::scalar_type(&column.type_),print::scalar_type(&joined)));
   144	                }
   145	            }
   146	        }
   147	        edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));for(start,end,value)in edits{text.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
   148	    }
   149	    if !domain_diagnostics.is_empty() {
   150	        let complete=super::release_bridge_current::prepare(target,current,&mut false);
   151	        if complete.diagnostics.is_empty(){return complete;}
   152	        let current_bridge=super::release_bridge_current::guarded(target,current);
   153	        if current_bridge.diagnostics.is_empty(){return current_bridge;}
   154	    }
   155	    // New concepts keep their current rules. They are retained populations,
   156	    // not a promise that a frozen old operation exposes a new concept.
   157	    let aliases:BTreeMap<_,_>=names.iter().filter_map(|old|new.get(old).filter(|(_,_,t)|&t.name!=old).map(|(_,_,t)|(t.name.clone(),old.clone()))).collect();
   158	    let mut additions=String::new();
   159	    for (name,(file,text,table)) in &new {
   160	        if name!=&table.name {continue;} // Alias lookup entries are not declarations.
   161	        if names.contains(name)||aliases.contains_key(name)||table.renamed_from.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|names.contains(old)){continue;}
   162	        affected.insert(name.clone());
   163	        match retained_declaration(file,text,table,&aliases) {
   164	            Ok(source)=>{additions.push_str(&source);additions.push('\n');},
   165	            Err(reason)=>domain_diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&table.site,reason,"retain an authored compatible guarded source for this table mapping").guide("deploy")),
   166	        }
   167	        changes.push(format!("new table {name}: retained with its current rules; old operations may not expose it"));
   168	    }
   169	    if !additions.is_empty() {let mut name="rollback-retained.q".to_owned();while sources.iter().any(|(f,_)|f==&name){name.insert_str(0,"retained-");}sources.push((name,additions));}
   170	    let transported=super::release_bridge_rules::carry(&mut sources,current,&mut changes,&std::collections::BTreeSet::new());
   171	    affected.extend(transported);
   172	    let (mut diagnostics,fallback)=super::release_bridge_guards::carry(&mut sources,current,&affected,&mut changes);
   173	    if !fallback.is_empty(){
   174	        super::release_bridge_rules::carry(&mut sources,current,&mut changes,&fallback);
   175	        (diagnostics,_)=super::release_bridge_guards::carry(&mut sources,current,&affected,&mut changes);
   176	    }
   177	    let current_bound=crate::compile(current);
   178	    if current_bound.diagnostics.is_empty()&&crate::compile(target).diagnostics.is_empty() {
   179	        super::release_bridge_indexes::prune_unused_equivalent_keys(&mut sources,current,&mut changes);
   180	    }
   181	    let bound=crate::compile(&sources);
   182	    if bound.schema.auth!=current_bound.schema.auth
   183	        || super::release_bridge_guards::current_context_needed(&sources,current,&bound.schema,&current_bound.schema) {
   184	        diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),"rollback guards need the current ordered execution and schema context","retain the complete current guarded workflow, including authentication and guard-dependent reads").guide("deploy"));
   185	    }
   186	    for(file,text)in target {
   187	        let old=parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
   188	        let Some((_,next))=current.iter().find(|(f,_)|operation_name(f,"")==operation_name(file,""))else{continue};
   189	        let next=parser::parse_document(file,next).0;
   190	        for(name,prior,site)in &old.aliases {
   191	            let qualified=operation_name(file,name);
   192	            let retained=current_bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==qualified);
   193	            if retained&&!next.aliases.iter().any(|(alias,target,_)|alias==name&&target==prior) {
   194	                diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(site,format!("rollback export {qualified} needs its current definition or alias target"),"retain the complete current guarded endpoint and dependency closure").guide("deploy"));
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    for previous in &crate::compile(target).programs {
   200	        if let Some(current)=current_bound.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==previous.program.name) {
   201	            let mut positions=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
   202	            let collapsed=previous.program.args.iter().any(|(name,_)| {
   203	                let spellings=std::iter::once(name.as_str()).chain(previous.program.arg_aliases.iter().filter(|(_,owner)|*owner==name).map(|(alias,_)|alias.as_str()));
   204	                let resolved=spellings.filter_map(|name|current.program.argument(name).map(|(canonical,_)|canonical)).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
   205	                resolved.len()>1||resolved.iter().any(|position|!positions.insert(*position))
   206	            });
   207	            if collapsed {
   208	                diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),format!("rollback export {} cannot merge or rebind old wire input positions behind current guards",previous.program.name),"author a compatible current guarded endpoint with distinct old argument positions").guide("deploy"));
   209	            }
   210	        }
   211	    }
   212	    let current_authority=match align_current_schema(&bound.schema,&sources,&current_bound.schema,current,&aliases) {
   213	        Ok(schema)=>Some(schema),
   214	        Err(error)=>{diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),format!("rollback current authority cannot align declared table/column identities: {error}"),"author a compatible guarded identity mapping").guide("deploy"));None},
   215	    };
   216	    for(name,table)in &bound.schema.tables {
   217	        let Some((_,_,next))=new.get(name)else{continue};
   218	        let Some(current_table)=current_bound.schema.table(&next.name)else{continue};
   219	        if current_authority.as_ref().is_some_and(|schema|schema.table(name).is_some_and(|authority| {
   220	            let policies=PolicyComparison::new(&bound.schema,schema);
   221	            table.column_reads.len()!=authority.column_reads.len()
   222	                ||table.column_reads.iter().any(|(column,rule)|!policies.optional_rule(Some(rule),authority.column_reads.get(column)))
   223	                ||table.written_by!=authority.written_by
   224	        })) {
   225	            diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&next.site,format!("rollback view needs current field authority on {name}"),"retain the complete current guarded workflow and its column read/write authority").guide("deploy"));
   226	        }
   227	        for column in &next.columns {
   228	            let old_name=column.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|name|table.columns.contains_key(*name)).unwrap_or(&column.name);
   229	            if table.columns.contains_key(old_name)&&table.defaults.get(old_name)!=current_table.defaults.get(&column.name) {
   230	                diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&column.site,format!("rollback view cannot preserve current default for {name}.{}",column.name),"retain the current authored default in a compatible guarded rollback source").guide("deploy"));
   231	            }
   232	        }
   233	    }
   234	    if bound.diagnostics.is_empty() && !super::release_bridge_current::contracts(&crate::compile(target),&bound) {
   235	        diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),"rollback view cannot preserve every predecessor wire contract, including argument aliases and distinct input positions","retain the old parameter aliases on compatible current endpoints, or author a guarded compatibility endpoint before deploying").guide("deploy"));
   236	    }
   237	    diagnostics.extend(bound.diagnostics);diagnostics.extend(domain_diagnostics);
   238	    Bridge {sources,changes,diagnostics}
   239	}
   240	fn offset(text:&str,line:u32,col:u32)->usize {
   241	    let start=text.split_inclusive('\n').take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(str::len).sum::<usize>();
   242	    start+text[start..].chars().take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(char::len_utf8).sum::<usize>()
   243	}
   244	pub fn type_span(file:&str,text:&str,column:&ast::ColumnDecl)->Option<(usize,usize)> {
   245	    let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);let at=tokens.iter().position(|t|t.site==column.site)?;
   246	    let start=tokens.get(at+2)?;let mut depth=0;
   247	    for (ordinal,token) in tokens.iter().skip(at+2).enumerate() {
   248	        // A nominal type may itself be named `default`, `read`, etc. Only
   249	        // recognize metadata after consuming the first token of the type.
   250	        if ordinal>0 && depth==0 && (token.kind==Kind::Newline || matches!(token.value.as_str(),"default"|"renamed"|"on"|"read"|"written"|"readable")) {

codex
Both the compiler field-authority gate and CLI admission call `align_current_schema` and use the shared policy comparison. I’m now checking how that map is constructed and how index declarations are transported and pruned; those steps determine whether the comparisons preserve the intended identities.

    14	fn line_span(file: &str, text: &str, site: &crate::Site) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
    15	    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, text);
    16	    let at = tokens.iter().position(|t| t.site == *site)?;
    17	    let end = tokens[at..].iter().find(|t| t.kind == Kind::Newline)?;
    18	    Some((
    19	        offset(text, site.line, 1),
    20	        offset(text, end.site.line, end.site.col),
    21	    ))
    22	}
    23	fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize, String) {
    24	    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
    25	    let mut end = text.len();
    26	    for (next, s) in text.lines().enumerate().skip(line as usize) {
    27	        if !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--") {
    28	            end = offset(text, next as u32 + 1, 1);
    29	            break;
    30	        }
    31	    }
    32	    (start, end, text[start..end].to_owned())
    33	}
    34	pub(super) fn carry(
    35	    sources: &mut [(String, String)],
    36	    current: &[(String, String)],
    37	    changes: &mut Vec<String>,
    38	    forced_programs: &BTreeSet<String>,
    39	) -> BTreeSet<String> {

    41	        .iter()

    48	        for table in &doc.tables {

    50	            if let Some(old) = &table.renamed_from {

    52	                tables.insert(old.clone(), table);
    53	            }
    54	        }

    59	    }

    61	    let before_schema=crate::compile(sources).schema;
    62	    let current_schema=crate::compile(current).schema.with_table_names(&aliases).ok();
    63	    let remap = |expr: &ast::Expr| {
    64	        let mut expr = expr.clone();
    65	        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr, &mut |e| {
    66	            if let ast::Expr::Read(r)
    67	            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }

    69	            {

    71	                    r.table = old.clone();
    72	                }
    73	            }

    75	        expr

    77	    let mut relaxed = BTreeSet::new();

    79	    for (file, text) in sources {
    80	        let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
    81	        let mut edits = Vec::new();

    87	            let authored_read=|rule:&ast::RuleDecl|->Option<String>{

    89	                let(a,b)=line_span(&rule.site.file,current_text,&rule.site)?;let raw=&current_text[a..b];
    90	                raw.trim_start().starts_with("read through ").then(||raw.to_owned())
    91	            };
    92	            // Replacement and insertion must use the same set: strengthening
    93	            // a named ordering to unique cannot leave two declarations behind.
    94	            let columns=super::release_bridge_indexes::column_names(old,next);
    95	            let same_key=|before:&ast::IndexDecl,after:&ast::IndexDecl| {
    96	                let Some(columns)=&columns else{return false};

    99	                }
   100	                // Opaque components are never inferred from their printed

   108	            let carried_indexes=next.indexes.iter().filter(|i|

   110	                || old.indexes.iter().any(|o|o.name==i.name&&o.unique!=i.unique)

   113	                .collect::<Vec<_>>();

   116	                if !loses_unique&&!carried_indexes.iter().any(|n|n.name==index.name){continue}

   134	                    }

   138	                    .iter()
   139	                    .find(|(_, _, d)| d.tables.iter().any(|t| t.name == next.name))
   140	                else {
   141	                    continue;
   142	                };

   144	                    let at = declaration(text, old.site.line).1;
   145	                    edits.push((at, at, format!("{}\n", &current_text[a..b])));
   146	                    expanded.insert(old.name.clone());
   147	                    changes.push(format!(
   148	                        "{}.{}: retained current guard/input field",
   149	                        old.name, column.name

   151	                }
   152	            }
   153	            if next.public.is_none(){if let Some((_,site))=&old.public {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}}
   154	            if old.public.is_none(){if let Some((_,site))=&next.public {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(&site.file,current_text,site){let at=declaration(text,old.site.line).1;edits.push((at,at,format!("{}\n",&current_text[a..b])));}}}
   155	            for current in &next.rules {

   173	                    ),
   174	                ));

   176	                    "{}: retained current {} authority",
   177	                    old.name,
   178	                    current.effect.word()
   179	                ));
   180	            }
   181	            // Constraints are current authority too: carry authored quotas,
   182	            // immutable fields and final-existence requirements verbatim.
   183	            for (_,site) in &old.immutable {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   184	            for cap in &old.caps {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,&cap.site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}

   192	            if let Some((field,_))=&next.not_before {edits.push((at,at,format!("  not before .{field}\n")));}
   193	            // An authored current endpoint or guard may name a new index. Keep
   194	            // that index with the body; an equal-key old name is not an alias.
   195	            for index in carried_indexes {

   225	                                };

   228	                                    print::expression(&remap(&current.value))

     1	//! Carry authored guards; never invent a value for a newly optional field.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — compilation gates automatic registration.
     3	use crate::{ast::Stmt, parser, print};
     4	use std::collections::BTreeSet;

     6	// Guard meaning depends on statement order and named definitions, not merely
     7	// the last textual binding. Compare declared row identities, then select
     8	// current execution when that guarded context cannot be transported intact.
     9	// Field/table authority is validated separately before choosing a strategy.
    10	pub(super) fn current_context_needed(
    11	    sources:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)],
    12	    view_schema:&snapback4_core::Schema,current_schema:&snapback4_core::Schema,
    13	)->bool {
    14	    let view_docs=sources.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    15	    let mut next_docs=current.iter().map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    16	    let mut names=current_schema.table_renames.iter().filter(|(_,old)|view_schema.tables.contains_key(*old)).map(|(new,old)|(new.clone(),old.clone())).collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    17	    for(name,prior)in &view_schema.table_renames {
    18	        if current_schema.tables.contains_key(prior)&&!current_schema.tables.contains_key(name){names.insert(prior.clone(),name.clone());}
    19	    }
    20	    for doc in &mut next_docs{normalize_identities(doc,&names);}
    21	    if super::release_bridge_indexes::current_context_needed(&view_docs,&next_docs,view_schema,current_schema,&names){return true;}
    22	    let exports=|docs:&[crate::ast::Document]| {
    23	        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    24	        for doc in docs {
    25	            for p in &doc.programs{out.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,&p.name),p.clone());}
    26	            for(name,target,_)in &doc.aliases{if let Some(p)=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target){out.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,name),p.clone());}}
    27	        }out
    28	    };
    29	    let shapes=|docs:&[crate::ast::Document]| {
    30	        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    31	        for doc in docs {
    32	            for shape in &doc.shapes {
    33	                let fields=shape.fields.iter().map(|field|match field {
    34	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Spread{..}=>"...".into(),
    35	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::NamedSpread{name,..}=>format!("...{name}"),
    36	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Bare{name,..}=>name.clone(),
    37	                    crate::ast::ShapeField::Field{name,value,..}=>format!("{name}:{}",expression_signature(value)),
    38	                }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    39	                out.insert(shape.name.clone(),fields);
    40	            }
    41	        }out
    42	    };
    43	    let shapes_changed=shapes(&view_docs)!=shapes(&next_docs);
    44	    let view=exports(&view_docs);let next=exports(&next_docs);
    45	    for(name,p)in next {
    46	        let Some(old)=view.get(&name)else{continue};
    47	        let mut sensitive=p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body).any(|s|matches!(s,Stmt::Require{..}|Stmt::If{..}|Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}));
    48	        let mut doc=crate::ast::Document::default();doc.programs.push(p.clone());
    49	        crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut doc,&mut |expr|{
    50	            sensitive|=matches!(expr,crate::ast::Expr::Read(_)|crate::ast::Expr::Exists{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Created{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Walk{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Merge(_)|crate::ast::Expr::If{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Assert{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Call{..}|crate::ast::Expr::Write{condition:Some(_),..});
    51	        });
    52	        let body=|p:&crate::ast::ProgramDecl|p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body).map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    53	        if sensitive&&(body(old)!=body(&p)||shapes_changed){return true;}
    54	    }
    55	    false
    56	}
    57	
    58	// Only typed row-reference slots change spelling. Literal values, member names,
    59	// helper identities and business strings retain their authored meaning.
    60	fn normalize_identities(doc:&mut crate::ast::Document,names:&std::collections::BTreeMap<String,String>) {
    61	    fn kind(ty:&mut crate::ast::ScalarType,names:&std::collections::BTreeMap<String,String>){
    62	        match &mut ty.kind {
    63	            crate::ast::TypeKind::Ref{table}=>if let Some(old)=names.get(table){*table=old.clone();},
    64	            crate::ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>kind(item,names),
    65	            _=>(),
    66	        }
    67	    }
    68	    for p in &mut doc.programs{for a in &mut p.args{kind(&mut a.type_,names);}}
    69	    for f in &mut doc.functions{for a in &mut f.args{kind(&mut a.type_,names);}}
    70	    let table=|name:&mut String|{if let Some(old)=names.get(name){*name=old.clone();}};
    71	    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(doc,&mut |expr|match expr {
    72	        crate::ast::Expr::Read(r)|crate::ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}|crate::ast::Expr::Created{read:r,..}=>table(&mut r.table),
    73	        crate::ast::Expr::Write{table:name,..}|crate::ast::Expr::Walk{table:name,..}=>table(name),
    74	        crate::ast::Expr::Merge(m)=>{table(&mut m.table);for lane in &mut m.lanes{if let Some(name)=&mut lane.table{table(name);}if let Some(r)=&mut lane.planned{table(&mut r.table);}if let Some((_,r))=&mut lane.nulls{table(&mut r.table);}}},
    75	        _=>(),
    76	    });
    77	}

    79	pub(super) fn carry(
    80	    sources: &mut [(String, String)],
    81	    current: &[(String, String)],
    82	    _affected: &BTreeSet<String>,
    83	    changes: &mut Vec<String>,
    84	) -> (Vec<crate::Diagnostic>,BTreeSet<String>) {
    85	    let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    86	    let mut fallback=BTreeSet::new();
    87	    let mut current_documents = current
    88	        .iter()
    89	        .map(|(file, text)| parser::parse_document(file, text).0)
    90	        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    91	    let target_documents = sources
    92	        .iter()
    93	        .map(|(file, text)| parser::parse_document(file, text).0)
    94	        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    95	    let target_tables=target_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).map(|t|t.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    96	    let current_tables=current_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables).map(|t|t.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    97	    let mut identities=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    98	    for table in current_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
    99	        if let Some(prior)=table.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|prior|target_tables.contains(*prior)){identities.insert(table.name.clone(),prior.clone());}
   100	    }
   101	    for table in target_documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
   102	        if let Some(prior)=table.renamed_from.as_ref().filter(|prior|current_tables.contains(*prior)&&!current_tables.contains(&table.name)){identities.insert(prior.clone(),table.name.clone());}
   103	    }
   104	    for doc in &mut current_documents{normalize_identities(doc,&identities);}
   105	    let helper_signatures = |docs: &[crate::ast::Document]| {
   106	        let mut out=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   107	        for doc in docs {
   108	            for f in &doc.functions {
   109	                let args=f.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),a.type_.clone(),a.alias.clone(),a.default.as_ref().map(expression_signature),a.deprecated)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   110	                let body=match &f.body {
   111	                    crate::ast::FunctionBody::Expression(e)=>vec![format!("expression {}",expression_signature(e))],
   112	                    crate::ast::FunctionBody::Block(body)=>body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect(),
   113	                };
   114	                let mut function=crate::ast::Document::default();function.functions.push(f.clone());
   115	                let mut calls=Vec::new();crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut function,&mut |e|if let crate::ast::Expr::Call{name,..}=e{calls.push(name.clone());});
   116	                out.insert(f.name.clone(),((args,body),calls));
   117	            }
   118	        }out
   119	    };
   120	    let current_helpers = helper_signatures(&current_documents);
   121	    let target_helpers = helper_signatures(&target_documents);
   122	    let mut next=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   123	    for doc in &current_documents {
   124	        for p in &doc.programs {next.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,&p.name),p.clone());}
   125	        for(name,target,_)in &doc.aliases {if let Some(p)=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target){let mut exported=p.clone();exported.name=name.clone();next.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(&doc.file,name),exported);}}
   126	    }
   127	    for (file, text) in sources {
   128	        let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
   129	        let mut edits = Vec::new();
   130	        for old in doc.programs {
   131	            let Some(new) = next.get(&super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name)) else {
   132	                continue;
   133	            };
   134	            // The whole selected source must retain current matching guards,
   135	            // including changes unrelated to the column that triggered a view.
   136	            let guards = old
   137	                .prelude
   138	                .iter()
   139	                .chain(&old.body)
   140	                .filter_map(guard)
   141	                .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   142	            let added = new
   143	                .prelude
   144	                .iter()
   145	                .chain(&new.body)
   146	                .filter_map(guard)
   147	                .filter(|g| !guards.contains(g))
   148	                .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   149	            let bindings = |p: &crate::ast::ProgramDecl| {
   150	                p.prelude
   151	                    .iter()
   152	                    .chain(&p.body)
   153	                    .filter_map(|s| {
   154	                        if let Stmt::Bind { names, value, .. } = s {
   155	                            Some((names, value))
   156	                        } else {
   157	                            None
   158	                        }
   159	                    })
   160	                    .flat_map(|(names, value)| {
   161	                        names.iter().enumerate().map(move |(at, name)| {
   162	                            (
   163	                                name.clone(),
   164	                                (format!("{at}:{}", expression_signature(value)), value.clone()),
   165	                            )
   166	                        })
   167	                    })
   168	                    .collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_, _>>()
   169	            };
   170	            let old_bindings = bindings(&old);
   171	            let new_bindings = bindings(new);
   172	            let mut dependent = BTreeSet::new();
   173	            let mut helpers = BTreeSet::new();
   174	            let collect = |expr: &crate::ast::Expr,
   175	                           names: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   176	                           helpers: &mut BTreeSet<String>| {
   177	                let mut expr = expr.clone();
   178	                crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr, &mut |e| match e {
   179	                    crate::ast::Expr::Name { name, .. } => {
   180	                        names.insert(name.clone());
   181	                    }
   182	                    crate::ast::Expr::Call { name, .. } => {
   183	                        helpers.insert(name.clone());
   184	                    }
   185	                    _ => (),
   186	                });
   187	            };
   188	            for s in new.prelude.iter().chain(&new.body) {
   189	                if let Stmt::Require { condition, .. } = s {
   190	                    collect(condition, &mut dependent, &mut helpers);
   191	                }
   192	            }
   193	            let mut visited = BTreeSet::new();
   194	            let mut unsafe_binding = false;
   195	            while let Some(name) = dependent.pop_first() {
   196	                if !visited.insert(name.clone()) {
   197	                    continue;
   198	                }
   199	                if let Some((signature, value)) = new_bindings.get(&name) {
   200	                    if old_bindings.get(&name).map(|(s, _)| s) != Some(signature)
   201	                        || has_write(value)
   202	                    {
   203	                        unsafe_binding = true;
   204	                    }
   205	                    collect(value, &mut dependent, &mut helpers);
   206	                } else if old_bindings.contains_key(&name) {
   207	                    unsafe_binding = true;
   208	                }
   209	            }
   210	            // Reused authored workflows retain their complete helper meaning,
   211	            // including helpers feeding writes, returns and system scopes.
   212	            let mut workflow = crate::ast::Document::default();
   213	            workflow.programs.push(new.clone());
   214	            crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut workflow, &mut |e| {
   215	                if let crate::ast::Expr::Call { name, .. } = e {
   216	                    helpers.insert(name.clone());
   217	                }
   218	            });
   219	            let mut visited = BTreeSet::new();
   220	            let mut unsafe_helper = false;
   221	            while let Some(name) = helpers.pop_first() {
   222	                // Helper declarations are global after the compiler validates
   223	                // optional file qualification. Compare the resolved identity.
   224	                let name=name.rsplit('.').next().unwrap_or(&name).to_owned();
   225	                if !visited.insert(name.clone()) {
   226	                    continue;
   227	                }
   228	                if current_helpers.get(&name).map(|s| &s.0)
   229	                    != target_helpers.get(&name).map(|s| &s.0)
   230	                {
   231	                    unsafe_helper = true;
   232	                }
   233	                if let Some((_, calls)) = current_helpers.get(&name) {
   234	                    helpers.extend(calls.iter().cloned());
   235	                }
   236	            }
   237	            let nested = |p: &crate::ast::ProgramDecl| {
   238	                p.body
   239	                    .iter()
   240	                    .filter(|s| matches!(s, Stmt::If { .. } | Stmt::For { .. } | Stmt::As { .. }))
   241	                    .map(|s| normalized_statement(s))
   242	                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
   243	            };
   244	            if unsafe_binding || unsafe_helper || nested(&old) != nested(new) {
   245	                fallback.insert(super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name));
   246	                diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&old.site,format!("rollback guard dependency closure for {} differs; an old binding/helper or control-flow path cannot establish the current guard",old.name),"retain a compatible guarded old workflow or declare intentional hiding of the unrepresentable values").guide("deploy"));
   247	                continue;
   248	            }
   249	            if added.is_empty() {
   250	                continue;
   251	            }
   252	            // Keep the old workflow; add current requirements after its reads,
   253	            // immediately before the first effect/return. Undefined or changed
   254	            // bindings fail ordinary compilation rather than guessing policy.
   255	            let at = old
   256	                .body
   257	                .iter()
   258	                .find(|s| {
   259	                    !matches!(s, Stmt::Require { .. })
   260	                        && !matches!(s,Stmt::Bind{value,..} if !has_write(value))
   261	                })
   262	                .map(stmt_site)
   263	                .unwrap_or(old.site.line + 1);
   264	            let offset = text
   265	                .split_inclusive('\n')
   266	                .take(at.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
   267	                .map(str::len)
   268	                .sum::<usize>();
   269	            edits.push((
   270	                offset,
   271	                added.iter().map(|g| format!("  {g}\n")).collect::<String>(),
   272	            ));
   273	            for g in added {
   274	                changes.push(format!("operation {}: carried current {g}", old.name));
   275	            }

    14	pub(super) fn prepare(target: &[Source], current: &[Source], guarded_fallback: &mut bool) -> Bridge {
    15	    *guarded_fallback=false;
    16	    let old = crate::compile(target);
    17	    let next = crate::compile(current);
    18	    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    19	        return Bridge {
    20	            sources: target.to_vec(),
    21	            changes: vec![],
    22	            diagnostics: old.diagnostics,
    23	        };
    24	    }
    25	    if !next.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    26	        return Bridge {
    27	            sources: current.to_vec(),
    28	            changes: vec![],
    29	            diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
    30	        };
    31	    }
    32	    if contracts(&old, &next) {
    33	        return retained(current.to_vec(), false);
    34	    }
    35	    match alpha(target, current) {
    36	        Ok(Some(sources)) => {
    37	            let bound = crate::compile(&sources);
    38	            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    39	                return Bridge {
    40	                    sources,
    41	                    changes: vec![],
    42	                    diagnostics: bound.diagnostics,
    43	                };
    44	            }
    45	            if contracts(&old, &bound) {
    46	                return retained(sources, true);
    47	            }
    48	        }
    49	        Err(error) => {*guarded_fallback=error.unsupported;return refused(current, &error.reason)},
    50	        Ok(None) => {}
    51	    }
    52	    *guarded_fallback=true;
    53	    refused(current,"the complete current workflow does not retain every predecessor table, field, operation and accepted input")
    54	}
    55	fn retained(sources: Vec<Source>, alpha: bool) -> Bridge {
    56	    Bridge {sources,diagnostics:vec![],changes:vec![format!("current independent intent: retained the complete current authored schema, defaults, programs, helpers, jobs and policy declarations{}; predecessor names and accepted inputs remain available; review current results in the old UI",if alpha {" under declared table identity renames"}else{""})]}
    57	}
    58	fn refused(current: &[Source], reason: &str) -> Bridge {
    59	    Bridge {sources:current.to_vec(),changes:vec![],diagnostics:vec![crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),format!("rollback view cannot preserve current independent intent: {reason}"),"author a compatible guarded source retaining the predecessor fields and inputs, truthful migrations and the current policy; an acknowledgment does not authorize restoring an unsafe workflow").guide("deploy")]}
    60	}
