    62	/// A loosened current workflow may omit a predecessor's nullable, default-free
    63	/// field rather than invent its value. All current schema and executable
    64	/// declarations stay intact; old wire aliases are the only source additions.
    65	pub(super) fn guarded(target:&[Source],current:&[Source])->Bridge {
    66	    let normalized=match alpha(target,current) {
    67	        Ok(sources)=>sources,
    68	        Err(error)=>return refused(current,&error.reason),
    69	    };
    70	    let current=normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(current);
    71	    let old=crate::compile(target);let next=crate::compile(current);
    72	    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty()||!next.diagnostics.is_empty(){return refused(current,"both authored sources must compile before preparing a guarded view");}
    73	    if !table_contracts(&old,&next,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow needs every predecessor table and required/defaulted field; author a truthful reverse projection or compatible guarded source");}
    74	    let mut sources=current.to_vec();let mut changes=vec!["guarded loosened view: retained the complete current schema, defaults, helpers, programs, jobs, aliases and independent intent; omitted nullable default-free predecessor fields are absent results, never invented values; review current results in the old UI".into()];
    75	    if normalized.is_some(){changes.push("declared table identities: complete current guarded workflow retained under predecessor spellings, without rewriting business strings or weakening authority".into());}
    76	    for(file,text)in &mut sources {
    77	        let doc=parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
    78	        let Some((prior_file,prior_text))=target.iter().find(|(f,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(f,"")==super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,""))else{continue};
    79	        let prior=parser::parse_document(prior_file,prior_text).0;
    80	        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);let mut edits=Vec::new();let mut extra=String::new();let mut restored=BTreeMap::new();
    81	        // Retain a missing old exported alias only to an authored current
    82	        // endpoint (including an explicitly authored current alias target).
    83	        // Existing/rebound current aliases remain exactly current-authored.
    84	        for(name,target,_)in &prior.aliases {
    85	            let qualified=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name);
    86	            if next.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==qualified){continue;}
    87	            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target).or_else(||doc.aliases.iter().find(|(alias,_,_)|alias==target).and_then(|(_,target,_)|doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)));
    88	            if let Some(endpoint)=endpoint{extra.push_str(&format!("alias {name} = {}\n",endpoint.name));restored.insert(qualified,endpoint.name.clone());}
    89	        }
    90	        for previous in &old.programs {
    91	            let qualified=&previous.program.name;
    92	            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&p.name)==*qualified).or_else(|| {
    93	                let target=doc.aliases.iter().find(|(name,_,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name)==*qualified).map(|(_,target,_)|target).or_else(||restored.get(qualified))?;
    94	                doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)
    95	            });
    96	            let Some(endpoint)=endpoint else{continue};
    97	            let actual=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&endpoint.name);
    98	            let Some(current)=next.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*qualified||p.program.name==actual)else{continue};
    99	            for(alias,owner)in &previous.program.arg_aliases {
   100	                if current.program.argument(alias).is_some(){continue;}
   101	                let Some((canonical,_))=current.program.argument(owner)else{continue};
   102	                let Some(arg)=endpoint.args.iter().find(|arg|arg.name==canonical&&arg.alias.is_none())else{continue};
   103	                if let Ok((_,end))=arg_type(text,&tokens,arg){edits.push((end,end,format!(" alias {alias}")));changes.push(format!("operation {qualified}: retained wire alias {alias} on {canonical}"));}
   104	            }
   105	        }
   106	        edits.sort();edits.dedup();edits.sort_by_key(|edit|std::cmp::Reverse(edit.0));
   107	        for(start,end,value)in edits{text.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
   108	        if !extra.is_empty(){if !text.ends_with('\n'){text.push('\n');}text.push_str(&extra);}
   109	    }
   110	    let bound=crate::compile(&sources);
   111	    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){return Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:bound.diagnostics};}
   112	    if !table_contracts(&old,&bound,true)||!endpoint_contracts(&old,&bound,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow cannot retain a predecessor query or a retained export's wire aliases and distinct inputs; author a compatible guarded endpoint with the old accepted arguments");}
   113	    for(name,table)in &old.schema.tables {
   114	        for column in table.columns.keys().filter(|column|!bound.schema.tables[name].columns.contains_key(*column)) {
   115	            if let Some(current)=renamed_column(&old,&bound,name,column){changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: declared identity is now {name}.{current}; guarded view uses the current spelling and result semantics; review the old UI"));}
   116	            else{changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: removed nullable default-free field remains absent in this guarded view; historical cells stay retained; review the old UI result"));}
   117	        }
   118	    }
   119	    for previous in &old.programs {
   120	        if !bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==previous.program.name){changes.push(format!("operation {}: paused because no compatible current authored write endpoint exists; old calls return E_OP; author a guarded compatibility endpoint if this old workflow is still needed",previous.program.name));}
   121	    }
   122	    Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:vec![]}
   123	}
   124	
   125	pub(super) fn contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound)->bool {
   126	    table_contracts(old,new,false)&&endpoint_contracts(old,new,false)
   127	}
   128	fn table_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,nullable_gaps:bool)->bool {
   129	    old.schema.tables.iter().all(|(name,table)|new.schema.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|next| {
   130	        table.columns.iter().all(|(column,kind)|next.columns.contains_key(column)
   131	            || (nullable_gaps&&renamed_column(old,new,name,column).is_some())
   132	            || (nullable_gaps&&kind.is_optional()&&!table.defaults.contains_key(column)&&!table.engine_field(column)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(column)))
   133	    }))
   134	}
   135	fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
   136	    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
   137	        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
   138	}
   139	pub(super) fn endpoint_contract(old:&snapback4_core::Program,new:&snapback4_core::Program)->bool {
   140	    if old.kind!=new.kind{return false;}
   141	    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();
   142	    for(name,kind)in &old.args {
   143	        let Some((target,next))=new.argument(name)else{return false};
   144	        if !accepts(kind,next)||!positions.insert(target){return false;}
   145	        if !old.arg_aliases.iter().filter(|(_,owner)|*owner==name).all(|(alias,_)|new.argument(alias).is_some_and(|(canonical,next)|canonical==target&&accepts(kind,next))){return false;}
   146	    }
   147	    new.args.iter().all(|(name,kind)|positions.contains(name.as_str())||matches!(kind,ArgType::Optional(_)))
   148	}
   149	fn renamed_column<'a>(old:&crate::Bound,new:&'a crate::Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->Option<&'a str> {
   150	    let next=new.schema.table(table)?;
   151	    if let Some((name,_))=new.schema.asset_renames.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|(name,prior)|prior.as_str()==column&&next.columns.contains_key(*name)){return Some(name.as_str());}
   152	    if let Some(prior)=old.schema.asset_renames.get(table).and_then(|m|m.get(column)) {
   153	        if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
   154	    }
   155	    for transform in &new.schema.backfills {
   156	        if transform.table==table&&transform.inputs.len()==1&&transform.inputs.contains_key(column)&&transform.outputs.len()==1 {
   157	            let name=transform.outputs.iter().next()?;
   158	            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{column}:{name}")&&next.columns.contains_key(name){return Some(name.as_str());}
   159	        }
   160	    }
   161	    for transform in &old.schema.backfills {
   162	        if transform.table==table&&transform.outputs.len()==1&&transform.outputs.contains(column)&&transform.inputs.len()==1 {
   163	            let prior=transform.inputs.keys().next()?;
   164	            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{prior}:{column}") {
   165	                if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
   166	            }
   167	        }
   168	    }
   169	    None
   170	}
   171	// This is inclusion of wire domains for an unchanged authored current body,
   172	// not synthesis of a conversion or permission to reinterpret old arguments.
   173	fn accepts(old: &ArgType, new: &ArgType) -> bool {
   174	    if old == new {
   175	        return true;
   176	    }
   177	    match (old, new) {
   178	        (ArgType::Optional(a), ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(a, b),
   179	        (_, ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(old, b),
   180	        (ArgType::Optional(_), _) => false,
   181	        (
   182	            ArgType::Text {
   183	                min: a,
   184	                max: b,
   185	                format: f,
   186	            },
   187	            ArgType::Text {
   188	                min: x,
   189	                max: y,
   190	                format: g,
   191	            },
   192	        ) => f == g && x <= a && b <= y,
   193	        (ArgType::Enum { variants: a }, ArgType::Enum { variants: b }) => {
   194	            a.iter().all(|v| b.contains(v))
   195	        }
   196	        (
   197	            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
   198	            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
   199	        ) => {
   200	            let parts = |kind: &ArgType| match kind {
   201	                ArgType::List { of, max } => (0, *max, of.clone()),
   202	                ArgType::ListRange { of, min, max } => (*min, *max, of.clone()),
   203	                _ => unreachable!(),
   204	            };
   205	            let (a, b, element) = parts(old);
   206	            let (x, y, next) = parts(new);
   207	            x <= a
   208	                && b <= y
   209	                && match (element, next) {
   210	                    (None, None) => true,
   211	                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => accepts(&a, &b),
   212	                    _ => false,
   213	                }
   214	        }
   215	        (ArgType::IntRange { .. }, ArgType::Int) => true,
   216	        (ArgType::IntRange { min: a, max: b }, ArgType::IntRange { min: x, max: y }) => {
   217	            x <= a && b <= y
   218	        }
   219	        _ => false,
   220	    }
   221	}
   222	fn word(name: &str) -> bool {
   223	    !name.is_empty() && name.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
   224	}
   225	fn offset(text: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> usize {
   226	    let start = text
   227	        .split_inclusive('\n')
   228	        .take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
   229	        .map(str::len)
   230	        .sum::<usize>();
   231	    start
   232	        + text[start..]
   233	            .chars()
   234	            .take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
   235	            .map(char::len_utf8)
   236	            .sum::<usize>()
   237	}
   238	fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize) {
   239	    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
   240	    let end = text
   241	        .split_inclusive('\n')
   242	        .enumerate()
   243	        .skip(line as usize)
   244	        .find(|(_, s)| {
   245	            !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--")
   246	        })
   247	        .map(|(i, _)| offset(text, i as u32 + 1, 1))
   248	        .unwrap_or(text.len());
   249	    (start, end)
   250	}
   251	fn remap_type(kind: &mut ast::ScalarType, names: &BTreeMap<String, String>) {
   252	    match &mut kind.kind {
   253	        TypeKind::Ref { table } => {
   254	            if let Some(name) = names.get(table) {
   255	                *table = name.clone()
   256	            }
   257	        }
   258	        TypeKind::List { item, .. } => remap_type(item, names),
   259	        _ => (),
   260	    }
   261	}
   262	// Closed, capture-avoiding alpha conversion of a single source module. Complex
   263	// declaration families require an explicitly authored compatible source.
   264	struct MappingError {reason:String,unsupported:bool}
   265	impl From<String> for MappingError {
   266	    fn from(reason:String)->Self{Self{reason,unsupported:false}}
   267	}
   268	impl From<&str> for MappingError {
   269	    fn from(reason:&str)->Self{reason.to_owned().into()}
   270	}
   271	fn alpha(target: &[Source], current: &[Source]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Source>>, MappingError> {
   272	    if target.len() != 1 || current.len() != 1 {
   273	        return Ok(None);
   274	    }
   275	    let old = parser::parse_document(&target[0].0, &target[0].1).0;
   276	    let (file, text) = &current[0];
   277	    let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
   278	    if old.tables.iter().chain(&doc.tables).any(|t| !word(&t.name)) {
   279	        return Ok(None);
   280	    }
   281	    let mut names = BTreeMap::new();
   282	    for next in &doc.tables {
   283	        if old.tables.iter().any(|t| t.name == next.name) {
   284	            continue;
   285	        }
   286	        if let Some(prior) = old.tables.iter().find(|t| {
   287	            next.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&t.name)
   288	                || t.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&next.name)
   289	        }) {
   290	            names.insert(next.name.clone(), prior.name.clone());
   291	        }
   292	    }
   293	    if names.is_empty() {
   294	        return Ok(None);
   295	    }
   296	    // A legal table name may also spell a primitive type. Reprinting that
   297	    // identity in an argument/column must never turn a nominal Ref into Int,
   298	    // Principal, etc. Validate the parser's actual type interpretation.
   299	    for name in names.values() {
   300	        let probe = format!("table __rollback_type_probe:\n  value: {name}\n");
   301	        let (parsed, diagnostics) = parser::parse_document("rollback-type-probe.q", &probe);
   302	        if !diagnostics.is_empty()
   303	            || !matches!(parsed.tables.first().and_then(|t|t.columns.first()).map(|c|&c.type_.kind),Some(TypeKind::Ref{table}) if table==name)
   304	        {
   305	            return Err(
   306	                "a table identity spelling reparses as a scalar or unsupported reference type"
   307	                    .into(),
   308	            );
   309	        }
   310	    }
   311	    let outputs = doc
   312	        .tables
   313	        .iter()
   314	        .map(|t| names.get(&t.name).unwrap_or(&t.name))
   315	        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   316	    if outputs.len() != doc.tables.len() || names.keys().any(|name| outputs.contains(name)) {
   317	        return Err(
   318	            "the declared table mapping is ambiguous or captures a current table name".into(),
   319	        );
   320	    }
   321	    let reserved = names
   322	        .keys()
   323	        .chain(names.values())
   324	        .cloned()
   325	        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   326	    let mut capture = doc.shapes.iter().any(|s| reserved.contains(&s.name));
   327	    capture |= doc.tables.iter().any(|t| {
   328	        t.rules
   329	            .iter()
   330	            .chain(t.column_reads.iter().map(|(_, r)| r))
   331	            .any(|r| r.bindings.iter().any(|n| reserved.contains(n)))
   332	    });
   333	    fn bindings(body: &[ast::Stmt], reserved: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
   334	        body.iter().any(|s| match s {
   335	            ast::Stmt::Bind { names, .. } => names.iter().any(|n| reserved.contains(n)),
   336	            ast::Stmt::For { name, body, .. } => {
   337	                reserved.contains(name) || bindings(body, reserved)
   338	            }
   339	            ast::Stmt::If {
   340	                then_body,
   341	                else_body,
   342	                ..
   343	            } => bindings(then_body, reserved) || bindings(else_body, reserved),
   344	            ast::Stmt::As { body, .. } => bindings(body, reserved),
   345	            _ => false,
   346	        })
   347	    }
   348	    capture |= doc
   349	        .programs
   350	        .iter()
   351	        .any(|p| bindings(&p.prelude, &reserved) || bindings(&p.body, &reserved));
   352	    capture |= doc
   353	        .functions
   354	        .iter()
   355	        .any(|f| matches!(&f.body,ast::FunctionBody::Block(body) if bindings(body,&reserved)));
   356	    for args in doc
   357	        .programs
   358	        .iter()
   359	        .map(|p| &p.args)
   360	        .chain(doc.functions.iter().map(|p| &p.args))
   361	        .chain(doc.actions.iter().map(|p| &p.args))
   362	    {
   363	        capture |= args.iter().any(|a| reserved.contains(&a.name));
   364	    }
   365	    let mut inspect = doc.clone();
   366	    let mut unsupported = false;
   367	    let mut check = |e: &mut ast::Expr| match e {
   368	        ast::Expr::Name { name, .. } => capture |= reserved.contains(name),
   369	        ast::Expr::Walk { .. } | ast::Expr::Merge(_) => unsupported = true,
   370	        _ => (),
   371	    };
   372	    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut inspect, &mut check);
   373	    for inv in &mut inspect.invariants {
   374	        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut inv.value, &mut check);
   375	    }
   376	    for table in &mut inspect.tables {
   377	        for rule in table
   378	            .rules
   379	            .iter_mut()
   380	            .chain(table.column_reads.iter_mut().map(|(_, r)| r))
   381	        {
   382	            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut rule.value, &mut check);
   383	        }
   384	        for column in &mut table.columns {
   385	            for value in [&mut column.read, &mut column.default]
   386	                .into_iter()
   387	                .flatten()
   388	            {
   389	                crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value, &mut check);
   390	            }
   391	        }
   392	        for (value, _) in &mut table.final_exists {
   393	            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value, &mut check);
   394	        }
   395	    }
   396	    if unsupported {
   397	        return Err(
   398	            "a recursive or merge expression needs an authored compatible table mapping".into(),
   399	        );
   400	    }
   401	    if capture {
   402	        return Err("a table identity mapping captures a local, argument or shape name".into());
   403	    }
   404	    if !doc.actions.is_empty()
   405	        || !doc.effects.is_empty()
   406	        || !doc.maintains.is_empty()
   407	        || !doc.timelines.is_empty()
   408	        || !doc.views.is_empty()
   409	        || !doc.feed_views.is_empty()
   410	        || !doc.backfills.is_empty()
   411	        || !doc.downfills.is_empty()
   412	        || !doc.extractions.is_empty()
   413	        || doc.programs.iter().any(|p| p.delivery.is_some())
   414	        || doc
   415	            .tables
   416	            .iter()
   417	            .any(|t| t.receipts.is_some() || t.columns.iter().any(|c| c.maintained.is_some()))
   418	    {
   419	        return Err(MappingError{reason:"this table identity mapping includes a derived, receipt or migration declaration outside the closed current-source adapter".into(),unsupported:true});
   420	    }
   421	    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, text);
   422	    let mut edits = Vec::<(usize, usize, String)>::new();
   423	    for table in &doc.tables {
   424	        let mut source = super::release_bridge::retained_declaration(file, text, table, &names)?;
   425	        if let Some(name) = names.get(&table.name) {
   426	            let prior = old
   427	                .tables
   428	                .iter()
   429	                .find(|t| &t.name == name)
   430	                .ok_or("missing predecessor identity")?;
   431	            source = header(
   432	                file,
   433	                &source,
   434	                &table.name,
   435	                name,
   436	                prior.renamed_from.as_deref(),
   437	            )?;
   438	        }
   439	        let (a, b) = declaration(text, table.site.line);
   440	        edits.push((a, b, source));
   441	    }
   442	    for inv in &doc.invariants {
   443	        let table = names.get(&inv.table).unwrap_or(&inv.table);
   444	        let mut value = inv.value.clone();
   445	        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |e| match e {
   446	            ast::Expr::Read(r)
   447	            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   448	            | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => {
   449	                if let Some(name) = names.get(&r.table) {
   450	                    r.table = name.clone()
   451	                }
   452	            }
   453	            ast::Expr::Write { table, .. } => {
   454	                if let Some(name) = names.get(table) {
   455	                    *table = name.clone()
   456	                }
   457	            }
   458	            _ => (),
   459	        });
   460	        let target = if let Some(column) = &inv.column {
   461	            format!("{table}.{column} {} only", inv.target)
   462	        } else if inv.target == "always" {
   463	            format!("{table} always")
   464	        } else {
   465	            format!("{table}.{} only", inv.target)
   466	        };
   467	        let (a, b) = declaration(text, inv.site.line);
   468	        edits.push((
   469	            a,
   470	            b,
   471	            format!("invariant {target} {}\n", print::expression(&value)),
   472	        ));
   473	    }
   474	    let mut other = doc.clone();
   475	    other.tables.clear();
   476	    other.invariants.clear();
   477	    let mut reference_error = None;
   478	    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut other, &mut |e| {
   479	        let (table, site) = match e {
   480	            ast::Expr::Read(r)
   481	            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   482	            | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => (&r.table, &r.site),
   483	            ast::Expr::Write { table, site, .. } => (&*table, &*site),
   484	            _ => return,
   485	        };
   486	        let Some(name) = names.get(table) else { return };
   487	        if let Some(token) = tokens.iter().find(|t| {
   488	            t.site.line == site.line
   489	                && t.site.col >= site.col
   490	                && t.kind == Kind::Word
   491	                && t.value == *table
   492	        }) {
   493	            let a = offset(text, token.site.line, token.site.col);
   494	            edits.push((a, a + token.value.len(), name.clone()));
   495	        } else {
   496	            reference_error = Some("a table reference has no unambiguous source span".to_owned());
   497	        }
   498	    });
   499	    if let Some(error) = reference_error {
   500	        return Err(error.into());
   501	    }
   502	    for arg in doc
   503	        .programs
   504	        .iter()
   505	        .flat_map(|p| &p.args)
   506	        .chain(doc.functions.iter().flat_map(|p| &p.args))
   507	        .chain(doc.actions.iter().flat_map(|p| &p.args))
   508	        .chain(doc.actions.iter().flat_map(|p| p.returns.iter().flatten()))
   509	    {
   510	        let mut kind = arg.type_.clone();
   511	        remap_type(&mut kind, &names);
   512	        if kind != arg.type_ {
   513	            let (a, b) = arg_type(text, &tokens, arg)?;
   514	            edits.push((a, b, print::scalar_type(&kind)));
   515	        }
   516	    }
   517	    edits.sort_by_key(|(a, b, _)| (*a, *b));
   518	    edits.dedup();
   519	    if edits.iter().any(|(a, b, _)| {
   520	        a > b || *b > text.len() || !text.is_char_boundary(*a) || !text.is_char_boundary(*b)
   521	    }) {
   522	        return Err("an unsupported source span needs an authored compatible mapping".into());
   523	    }
   524	    if edits.windows(2).any(|p| p[0].1 > p[1].0) {
   525	        return Err("overlapping source spans in table identity mapping".into());
   526	    }
   527	    let mut result = text.clone();
   528	    for (a, b, value) in edits.into_iter().rev() {
   529	        result.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   530	    }
   531	    Ok(Some(vec![(file.clone(), result)]))
   532	}
   533	fn header(
   534	    file: &str,
   535	    source: &str,
   536	    from: &str,
   537	    to: &str,
   538	    prior: Option<&str>,
   539	) -> Result<String, String> {
   540	    if prior.is_some_and(|p| !word(p)) {
   541	        return Err("qualified predecessor table origin needs an authored source".into());
   542	    }
   543	    let line = source.lines().next().ok_or("missing table header")?;
   544	    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, line);
   545	    let name = tokens
   546	        .get(1)
   547	        .filter(|t| t.kind == Kind::Word && t.value == from)
   548	        .ok_or("missing table name span")?;
   549	    let mut edits = vec![(
   550	        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col),
   551	        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col) + from.len(),
   552	        to.to_owned(),
   553	    )];
   554	    if let Some(at) = tokens.iter().enumerate().skip(2).find_map(|(i,t)| (t.value=="renamed").then_some(i)) {
   555	        let last = tokens
   556	            .get(at + 2)
   557	            .filter(|_| tokens.get(at + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "from"))
   558	            .ok_or("invalid table origin span")?;
   559	        edits.push((
   560	            offset(line, tokens[at].site.line, tokens[at].site.col),
   561	            offset(line, last.site.line, last.site.col) + last.value.len(),
   562	            prior
   563	                .map(|p| format!("renamed from {p}"))
   564	                .unwrap_or_default(),
   565	        ));
   566	    } else if let Some(prior) = prior {
   567	        let colon = tokens
   568	            .iter()
   569	            .find(|t| t.value == ":")
   570	            .ok_or("missing table header colon")?;
   571	        let a = offset(line, colon.site.line, colon.site.col);
   572	        edits.push((a, a, format!(" renamed from {prior}")));
   573	    }
   574	    let mut out = source.to_owned();
   575	    edits.sort_by_key(|(a, _, _)| std::cmp::Reverse(*a));
   576	    for (a, b, value) in edits {
   577	        out.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   578	    }
   579	    Ok(out)
   580	}
   581	pub(super) fn arg_type(
   582	    text: &str,
   583	    tokens: &[lexer::Token],
   584	    arg: &ast::ArgDecl,
   585	) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
   586	    let at = tokens
   587	        .iter()
   588	        .position(|t| t.site == arg.site)
   589	        .ok_or("missing argument source span")?;
   590	    let colon = (at..tokens.len())
   591	        .find(|i| tokens[*i].value == ":")
   592	        .ok_or("missing argument type colon")?;
   593	    let start = tokens.get(colon + 1).ok_or("missing argument type")?;
   594	    let mut depth = 0_i32;
   595	    for (ordinal, t) in tokens.iter().skip(colon + 1).enumerate() {
   596	        // `alias` and `default` are legal first nominal type tokens too.
   597	        if ordinal > 0
   598	            && depth == 0
   599	            && (matches!(
   600	                t.value.as_str(),
   601	                "," | ")" | "=" | "default" | "alias" | "renamed" | "deprecated"
   602	            ) || t.kind == Kind::Newline)
   603	        {
   604	            return Ok((
   605	                offset(text, start.site.line, start.site.col),
   606	                text[..offset(text, t.site.line, t.site.col)]
   607	                    .trim_end()
   608	                    .len(),
   609	            ));
   610	        }
   611	        match t.value.as_str() {
   612	            "(" | "[" | "<" => depth += 1,
   613	            ")" | "]" | ">" => depth -= 1,
   614	            _ => (),
   615	        }
   616	    }
   617	    Err("unterminated argument type source span".into())
   618	}

//! Compare final-existence obligations in declared row/column identity space.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — spelling-only changes must not trigger a view.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::schema::{Constraint, RuleTerm, Schema};
use crate::parser;

#[derive(Default)]
struct Identities {
    tables: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    columns: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
}
impl Identities {
    fn table(&self, name: &str) -> String {self.tables.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
    fn column(&self, table: &str, name: &str) -> String {
        self.columns.get(table).and_then(|c|c.get(name)).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())
    }
    fn term(&self, source: &str, term: &RuleTerm) -> RuleTerm {
        match term {
            RuleTerm::Field(c)=>RuleTerm::Field(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::OldField(c)=>RuleTerm::OldField(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>RuleTerm::NextField(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::Reference{table,column,field,old}=>RuleTerm::Reference{
                table:self.table(table),column:self.column(source,column),field:self.column(table,field),old:*old},
            RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,hops,old}=>RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{
                column:self.column(source,column),old:*old,hops:hops.iter().map(|h|snapback4_core::schema::RuleHop{
                    table:self.table(&h.table),field:self.column(&h.table,&h.field)}).collect()},
            RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)=>term.clone(),
        }
    }
    fn signature(&self,schema:&Schema,source:&str,constraint:&Constraint)->Option<(String,Vec<String>,Vec<RuleTerm>)> {
        let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=constraint else{return None};
        let index=schema.table(table)?.index(index)?;
        if !index.is_plain(){return None;}
        Some((self.table(table),index.columns().iter().map(|c|self.column(table,c)).collect(),
            key.iter().map(|t|self.term(source,t)).collect()))
    }
}

pub(super) fn relaxed(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    let old=target.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if !old.iter().any(|t|!t.final_exists.is_empty()){return false;}
    let next=current.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let (before,after)=(crate::compile(target),crate::compile(current));
    if !before.diagnostics.is_empty()||!after.diagnostics.is_empty(){return true;}
    let mut identities=Identities::default();
    let mut pairs=Vec::new();
    for prior in &old {
        let Some(now)=next.iter().find(|n|n.name==prior.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&prior.name)||prior.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name))else{continue};
        identities.tables.insert(now.name.clone(),prior.name.clone());
        let mut columns=BTreeMap::new();
        for c in &prior.columns {
            if let Some(n)=now.columns.iter().find(|n|n.name==c.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&c.name)||c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name)) {
                columns.insert(n.name.clone(),c.name.clone());
            }
        }
        if now.columns.iter().map(|c|columns.get(&c.name).unwrap_or(&c.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=now.columns.len(){return true;}
        identities.columns.insert(now.name.clone(),columns);
        pairs.push((prior.name.as_str(),now.name.as_str()));
    }
    // Never let two independent current identities collapse to one old name.
    if next.iter().map(|t|identities.table(&t.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=next.len(){return true;}
    let unchanged=Identities::default();
    for (prior,now) in pairs {
        let (Some(old_table),Some(new_table))=(before.schema.table(prior),after.schema.table(now))else{return true};
        for constraint in &old_table.constraints {
            if !matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{..}){continue;}
            let Some(expected)=unchanged.signature(&before.schema,prior,constraint)else{return true};
            if !new_table.constraints.iter().any(|c|identities.signature(&after.schema,now,c).as_ref()==Some(&expected)){return true;}
        }
    }
    false
}

    79	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_values")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET column_name=?3 WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![t,old,new])?;}
    80	            if exists_table(conn,"_dormant_columns")? {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_columns SET column_name=?3 WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2",params![name,old,new])?;}
    81	        }
    82	        if !map.is_empty(){
    83	            for rows in ["_dormant_narrow_rows","_dormant_tables"] {
    84	                if !exists_table(conn,rows)?{continue;}
    85	                let condition=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{"table_name=?1"}else{"t=?1"};
    86	                let value=if rows=="_dormant_tables"{rusqlite::types::Value::Text(name.clone())}else{rusqlite::types::Value::Integer(*t)};
    87	                let definitions:Vec<String>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT DISTINCT definition FROM {rows} WHERE {condition}"))?.query_map([&value],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    88	                for text in definitions {let mut definition:Table=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;table(&mut definition,&map);conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {rows} SET definition=?2 WHERE {condition} AND definition=?3"),params![value,canonical_json(&definition),text])?;}
    89	            }
    90	            maps.insert(name.clone(),map);
    91	        }
    92	    }
    93	    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[super::table_renames::VERSION])?;
    94	    Ok(maps)
    95	}
    96	fn exists_table(conn:&Connection,name:&str)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get(0))?)}
    97	fn asset(kind:&crate::schema::ColumnType)->bool {use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;match kind{Optional(k)|List{of:k,..}|ListRange{of:k,..}=>asset(k),File{..}|Image{..}|Video{..}|Ref{..}=>true,_=>false}}

     1	//! @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — future-write risks are distinct from snapshot validation.
     2	use snapback4_core::{Schema, successor::{self, Change}, schema::ColumnDefault};
     3	use std::path::Path;
     4	
     5	pub(super) fn missing_defaults(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema)->Result<Vec<String>,String>{
     6	    let names=candidate.table_renames.iter().filter(|(new,prior)|old.table(prior).is_some()&&old.table(new).is_none()).map(|(new,prior)|(prior.clone(),new.clone())).collect();
     7	    let old=old.with_table_names(&names).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
     8	    let mut missing=Vec::new();
     9	    for(name,target)in old.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none()){
    10	        let Some(from)=candidate.table(name)else{continue};if old.maintain_for(name).is_some(){continue}
    11	        for(column,kind)in &target.columns {
    12	            let renamed=candidate.asset_renames.get(name).is_some_and(|map|map.iter().any(|(new,prior)|prior==column&&from.columns.contains_key(new)));
    13	            if !target.engine_field(column)&&!snapback4_core::schema::timeline::is_slot(column)&&!target.maintained.contains_key(column)&&matches!(target.defaults.get(column),Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(_)))&&!from.columns.contains_key(column)&&!renamed&&!projected(candidate,name,column,kind){missing.push(format!("{name}.{column}"));}
    14	        }
    15	    }Ok(missing)
    16	}
    17	
    18	pub(super) fn risks(candidate: &Schema, old: &Schema, release: u64, dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    19	    risks_for(candidate,old,release,dir,true,true)
    20	}
    21	// Timeline event slots are compiler-owned delivery metadata, not authored old
    22	// cells. Retiring a rebuildable feed must not manufacture a visibility view.
    23	pub(super) fn old_row_risks(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema,release:u64,dir:&Path)->Vec<String>{
    24	    risks_for(candidate,old,release,dir,false,false)
    25	}
    26	fn risks_for(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema,release:u64,dir:&Path,include_new_tables:bool,include_constraints:bool)->Vec<String>{
    27	    let names:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=candidate.table_renames.iter().filter(|(new,prior)|old.table(prior).is_some() && old.table(new).is_none()).map(|(new,prior)|(prior.clone(),new.clone())).collect();
    28	    if names.is_empty(){return risks_aligned(candidate,old,release,dir,include_new_tables,include_constraints)}
    29	    let aligned=match old.with_table_names(&names){Ok(schema)=>schema,Err(error)=>return vec![format!("rollback foresight cannot compare renamed identities: {error}; run exact rollback --check")]};
    30	    risks_aligned(candidate,&aligned,release,dir,include_new_tables,include_constraints).into_iter().map(|line|{
    31	        let (mut facts,command)=line.split_once("; run `").map_or((line.clone(),String::new()),|(facts,command)|(facts.to_owned(),format!("; run `{command}")));
    32	        for (prior,new) in &names {facts=facts.replace(&format!(", {new}."),&format!(", {prior} (current {new}).")).replace(&format!("table {new}:"),&format!("table {prior} (current {new}):")).replace(&format!("unique index {new}."),&format!("unique index {prior} (current {new})."));}
    33	        facts+&command
    34	    }).collect()
    35	}
    36	fn risks_aligned(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema,release:u64,dir:&Path,include_new_tables:bool,include_constraints:bool)->Vec<String>{
    37	    let mut risks:Vec<_>=successor::classify(candidate,old).into_iter().filter_map(|step| {
    38	        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.")?;
    39	        let (table,column)=path.split_once(".columns.")?;
    40	        let target=old.tables.get(table)?;
    41	        if target.engine_field(column) || snapback4_core::schema::timeline::is_slot(column) || old.maintain_for(table).is_some() || target.maintained.contains_key(column) {return None;}
    42	        let before=target.columns.get(column)?;
    43	        if projected(candidate,table,column,before) {return None;}
    44	
    45	        let from=candidate.tables.get(table)?;
    46	        let source_kind=from.columns.get(column).or_else(||candidate.asset_renames.get(table)?.iter().find(|(_,old)|old.as_str()==column).and_then(|(new,_)|from.columns.get(new)));
    47	        let reason=match source_kind {
    48	            None if !before.is_optional() && !matches!(target.defaults.get(column),Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(_))) =>
    49	                "without a fitting reverse projection, new rows will lack the old required field, so rollback can refuse".to_owned(),
    50	            Some(kind) if kind!=before && matches!(step.change,Change::Narrowing|Change::Destructive) =>
    51	                format!("new writes allowed by {kind} may not fit {before}, so rollback can hide rows; {}", remedy(before)),
    52	            _ => return None,
    53	        };
    54	        Some(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, {table}.{column}: {reason}; keep an expand/contract bridge with the old required fields and compatible domains; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()))
    55	    }).collect();
    56	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#19-dormant-visibility-and-migration-dorm — warn before new writes, including harmless newer-only facts and empty legacy cells.
    57	    for (name, _) in candidate.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none() && candidate.maintain_for(name).is_none()) {
    58	        if include_new_tables && old.table(name).is_none() && !candidate.table_renames.get(name).is_some_and(|source|old.table(source).is_some()) {
    59	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: rows written to new table {name} will be hidden while release {release} is live; release {release} has no such table, so these are newer-only facts, not lost old-table records; a same-table `down` cannot create this missing table; keep it in a loosened bridge or declare a table rename; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    60	        }
    61	    }
    62	    for (name,target) in old.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
    63	        let Some(from)=candidate.tables.get(name) else {continue;};
    64	        if old.maintain_for(name).is_some() {continue;}
    65	        for (column,kind) in &target.columns {
    66	            if projected(candidate,name,column,kind) || from.columns.contains_key(column) || target.engine_field(column) || snapback4_core::schema::timeline::is_slot(column) || target.maintained.contains_key(column) {continue;}
    67	            let interpretation=if kind.is_optional() {"null".to_owned()} else if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value))=target.defaults.get(column) {format!("the old default {}",snapback4_core::wire::to_json(value))} else {continue;};
    68	            let suggestions=cross_table_suggestions(candidate,old,name,column,kind,target.defaults.get(column));
    69	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, {name}.{column}: without a fitting reverse projection, records created while this field is absent have no retained old cell and will show {interpretation} to release {release} users; declare a reverse projection from the replacement value, or retain the field in a bridge release; {suggestions}run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    70	        }
    71	        // Registration compares constraint domains structurally in
    72	        // release_bridge::requires_view (including renamed fields). General
    73	        // foresight advice also explains constraint changes, but is not a
    74	        // second, less precise admission predicate.
    75	        if include_constraints {
    76	        for (index,definition) in &target.indexes {
    77	            if definition.unique && from.indexes.get(index)!=Some(definition) {
    78	                risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, unique index {name}.{index}: future writes may introduce duplicate keys and refuse restoration; keep the old uniqueness in a bridge; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    79	            }
    80	        }
    81	        if target.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| {
    82	            if from.constraints.contains(constraint){return false;}
    83	            match constraint {
    84	                snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Text{field,..}=>!target.columns.get(field).is_some_and(|kind|projected(candidate,name,field,kind)),
    85	                snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Cap{by,max}=>!from.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Cap{by:next_by,max:next_max}if next_by==by&&next_max<=max)),
    86	                snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{..}=>true,
    87	                _=>false,
    88	            }
    89	        }) {
    90	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, table {name}: changed constraints may let future writes refuse rollback; preserve old bounds/quotas in a bridge; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    91	        }
    92	        }
    93	    }
    94	    risks
    95	}
    96	
    97	fn projected(candidate:&Schema,table:&str,column:&str,kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType)->bool {
    98	    candidate.extractions.iter().any(|e|e.parent==table&&e.fields.values().any(|c|c==column)) || (scalar(kind) && candidate.downfills.iter().any(|f|f.table==table && f.outputs.contains(column))) || candidate.asset_renames.get(table).is_some_and(|map|map.iter().any(|(new,old)|old==column && candidate.tables.get(table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(new))==Some(kind)))
    99	}
   100	fn scalar(kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType)->bool {
   101	    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::*;
   102	    match kind {Optional(k)=>scalar(k),Image{..}|Video{..}|File{..}|List{..}|ListRange{..}|Ref{..}=>false,_=>true}
   103	}
   104	fn remedy(kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType)->&'static str {
   105	    if scalar(kind) {"declare a truthful scalar `down`, or generate a loosened bridge"}
   106	    else {"computed `down` cannot project files, images, collections or references; generate a loosened bridge, or keep the required value and add a status"}
   107	}

   109	#[cfg(test)]
   110	mod tests {
   111	    use super::*;
   112	    fn schema(source:&str)->Schema {
   113	        let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
   114	        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b.schema
   115	    }
   116	    #[test]
   117	    fn warns_before_any_future_rows_exist_but_compatible_additions_are_quiet() {
   118	        let base="table items:\n  required: int 0..10\n  state: enum('old')\n  public\n";
   119	        let old=schema(base);
   120	        for (candidate,subject) in [
   121	            (base.replace("  required: int 0..10\n",""),"items.required"),
   122	            (base.replace("int 0..10","int 0..20"),"items.required"),
   123	            (base.replace("enum('old')","enum('old','new')"),"items.state"),
   124	            (base.replace("int 0..10","int 0..10 ?"),"items.required"),
   125	        ] {
   126	            let warnings=risks(&schema(&candidate),&old,1,Path::new("deployment"));
   127	            assert!(warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains(subject)&&w.contains("--to 1 --check --bridge")),"{warnings:?}");
   128	        }
   129	        assert!(risks(&schema(&base.replace("  public","  label: text <=20 ?\n  public")),&old,1,Path::new("deployment")).is_empty());
   130	        let unique=schema(&base.replace("  public","  unique state\n  public"));
   131	        assert!(risks(&old,&unique,1,Path::new("deployment")).iter().any(|w|w.contains("duplicate keys")));
   132	    }
   133	    #[test]
   134	    fn warns_about_newer_only_tables_and_empty_legacy_cells_before_any_writes() {
   135	        let old=schema("table items:\n  reason: text <=40 ?\n  public\n");
   136	        let next=schema("table items:\n  public\ntable explanations:\n  text: text <=40\n  public\n");
   137	        let warnings=risks(&next,&old,1,Path::new("deployment"));
   138	        assert!(warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains("new table explanations") && w.contains("newer-only facts")),"{warnings:?}");
   139	        assert!(warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains("items.reason") && w.contains("show null") && w.contains("reverse projection")),"{warnings:?}");
   140	    }
   141	    #[test]
   142	    fn declared_down_and_scalar_or_asset_renames_do_not_warn_about_absent_fields() {
   143	        let old=schema("table items:\n  state: enum('old')\n  public\n");
   144	        let new=schema("table items:\n  state: enum('old','new')\n  public\ndown items oldState(state: enum('old','new')): {state:'old'}\n");
   145	        assert!(risks(&new,&old,1,Path::new("deployment")).is_empty());
   146	        for kind in ["text <=40 ?","file <=1024 ?"] {
   147	            let old=schema(&format!("table items:\n  title: {kind}\n  public\n"));
   148	            let new=schema(&format!("table items:\n  name: {kind} renamed from title\n  public\n"));
   149	            assert!(risks(&new,&old,1,Path::new("deployment")).is_empty(),"{kind}");
   150	        }
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    #[test]
   154	    fn table_rename_still_warns_about_a_loosened_required_old_field() {
   155	        let old=schema("table orders:\n  n: int\n  public\n");
   156	        let new=schema("table records renamed from orders:\n  n: int ?\n  public\n");
   157	        let warnings=risks(&new,&old,1,Path::new("deployment"));
   158	        assert!(warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains("orders (current records).n")&&w.contains("hide rows")),"{warnings:?}");
   159	        assert!(!warnings.iter().any(|w|w.contains("new table records")),"{warnings:?}");
   160	    }

   163	
   164	fn cross_table_suggestions(candidate:&Schema,old:&Schema,parent:&str,column:&str,kind:&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType,default:Option<&ColumnDefault>)->String {
   165	    let fallback=match default {Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(v))=>match v{snapback4_core::Value::Bool(v)=>v.to_string(),snapback4_core::Value::Int(v)=>v.to_string(),snapback4_core::Value::String(v)=>snapback4_lang::print::quote(v),_=>return String::new()},_ if kind.is_optional()=>"null".into(),_=>return String::new()};
   166	    let mut proposals=Vec::new();
   167	    for(child,table)in candidate.visible_tables().filter(|(name,_)|old.table(name).is_none()) {
   168	        for(reference,ty)in &table.columns {
   169	            if !matches!(ty.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{table} if table==parent){continue}
   170	            let values=table.columns.iter().filter(|(c,k)|!table.engine_field(c)&&c.as_str()!=reference.as_str()&&k.required()==kind.required()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   171	            let unique=table.indexes.values().any(|i|i.unique&&i.columns()==vec![reference.clone()]);
   172	            if unique&&values.len()==1 {
   173	                let value=values[0].0;
   174	                proposals.push(format!("candidate cross-table down (confirm the replacement field's meaning): `down {parent} rollback{column}(id: id): {{{column}: {child}[{reference} = id].{value} ?? {fallback}}}`"));
   175	            }else if kind.required()==&snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Bool {
   176	                proposals.push(format!("if child existence exactly means {column}, candidate cross-table down: `down {parent} rollback{column}(id: id): {{{column}: exists {child} where .{reference} = id}}`"));
   177	            }
   178	        }
   179	    }
   180	    if proposals.is_empty(){String::new()}else{proposals.join("; ")+"; "}
   181	}

    64	def witness(h, pg_url):
    65	    work, cli, start, stop, http = (h[k] for k in ('WORK','cli','start','stop','http'))
    66	    transcripts = {}
    67	    for backend, target in [('postgres', pg_url), ('sqlite', None)]:
    68	        deployment = work / ('ev1-' + backend)
    69	        records = []
    70	        assert V3 != V1 and 'format url ?' in V3
    71	        for version, source in [(1,V1),(2,V2),(3,V3)]:
    72	            (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(source)
    73	            args = ['deploy', str(deployment)]
    74	            if version == 3: args += ['--restore-dormant']
    75	            cli(args, url=target)
    76	            child, base = start('ev1-'+backend+'-v'+str(version),target,deployment=deployment)
    77	            guest = http(base,'POST','/auth/guest',{})['session']
    78	            token = guest['token']
    79	            state = http(base,'GET','/schema',token=token)
    80	            assert state['generation']==version,state
    81	            def capture(label, value):
    82	                # Each backend mints its own guest principal. Check its value
    83	                # before excluding that nondeterministic identity from comparison.
    84	                if ' q ' in label: assert value['viewer']==guest['principal'],value
    85	                if 'store_id' in value: assert value['store_id']==state['store_id'],value
    86	                independent = {k:v for k,v in value.items() if k not in ('store_id','now','viewer')}
    87	                records.append((label,json.dumps(independent,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))))
    88	                return value
    89	            def mutate(name, identity, args):
    90	                return capture(f'v{version} m {name}',http(base,'POST','/m/'+name,{'id':identity,'args':args,'store_id':state['store_id']},token))
    91	            def query(name, args):
    92	                return capture(f'v{version} q {name}',http(base,'POST','/q/'+name,{'args':args},token))
    93	            if version == 1:
    94	                assert mutate('put','ev1-a',{'key':'a','n':2,'memo':'雪\0exact','site':'https://example.com'})['state']=='sent'
    95	                assert mutate('put','ev1-b',{'key':'b','n':1,'memo':None,'site':None})['state']=='sent'
    96	                assert mutate('archive','ev1-z',{'key':'z','text':'retained row'})['state']=='sent'
    97	                first = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']
    98	                archived = query('archived',{'key':'z'})['data']
    99	                assert [r['n'] for r in query('ordered',{})['data']]==[1,2]
   100	            elif version == 2:
   101	                changed = query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']
   102	                assert changed == {'id':'a','n':2,'extra':None,'count':7},changed
   103	                assert len(query('ordered',{})['data'])==2
   104	                assert mutate('add','ev1-new',{'key':'new','n':9})['state']=='sent'
   105	                assert query('addedRow',{'key':'new'})['data']['n']==9
   106	            else:
   107	                assert query('peek',{'key':'a'})['data']==dict(first,extra=None,count=7)
   108	                assert query('archived',{'key':'z'})['data']==archived
   109	                assert [r['n'] for r in query('ordered',{})['data']]==[1,2]
   110	            stop(child)
   111	            if backend == 'postgres':
   112	                deadline=time.monotonic()+15
   113	                while True:
   114	                    doctor=json.loads(cli(['doctor','--release',str(deployment),'--json'],url=target).stdout)
   115	                    if doctor['store']['expired']: break
   116	                    assert time.monotonic()<deadline,doctor
   117	                    time.sleep(.1)
   118	        transcripts[backend]=json.dumps(records,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')).encode()
   119	        (work/('ev1-'+backend+'-wire.json')).write_bytes(transcripts[backend])
   120	        print('P1-EV-1',backend,'PASS: v1 writes, v2 additions/retirements/indexes, v3 exact restoration',flush=True)
   121	    assert transcripts['postgres']==transcripts['sqlite'], 'EV-1 wire bytes differ'
   122	    (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(h['source'])
   123	    print('P1-EV-1 PASS: byte-identical wire after removing store_id, now and the checked minted guest viewer',flush=True)
   124	    large_default_witness(h, pg_url.replace('schema=ev1', 'schema=ev1_large'))

   127	def large_default_witness(h, pg_url):
   128	    """A restored raw parent image must widen /changes identically on both stores."""
   129	    work, cli, start, stop, http = (h[k] for k in ('WORK','cli','start','stop','http'))
   130	    v1 = '''use identity
   131	table notes:
   132	  n: int
   133	  memo: json <=100 ?
   134	  public
   135	  insert <- allow
   136	  delete <- allow
   137	  online only
   138	table children:
   139	  note: notes on delete restrict
   140	  public
   141	  insert <- allow
   142	  online only
   143	mutation put(key: id):
   144	  insert notes {id: key, n: 1}
   145	mutation erase(key: id):
   146	  delete notes[key]
   147	query peek(key: id):
   148	  return notes[key]
   149	'''
   150	    sibling = "  payload: text <=307200 default '" + 'x' * (300 * 1024) + "'\n"
   151	    versions = [v1, v1.replace('  memo: json <=100 ?\n', sibling),
   152	        v1.replace('  memo: json <=100 ?\n', '  memo: json <=100 ?\n' + sibling)]
   153	    answers = {}
   154	    for backend, target in [('postgres', pg_url), ('sqlite', None)]:
   155	        deployment = work / ('ev1-large-' + backend)
   156	        for version, source in enumerate(versions, 1):
   157	            (work/'app/snapback/schema.q').write_text(source)
   158	            cli(['deploy', str(deployment)] + (['--restore-dormant'] if version == 3 else []), url=target)
   159	            child, base = start(f'ev1-large-{backend}-v{version}', target, deployment=deployment)
   160	            guest = http(base, 'POST', '/auth/guest', {})['session']
   161	            token = guest['token']
   162	            state = http(base, 'GET', '/schema', token=token)
   163	            assert state['generation'] == version, state
   164	            def mutate(name):
   165	                return http(base, 'POST', '/m/'+name, {'id':f'large-{name}',
   166	                    'args':{'key':'a'}, 'store_id':state['store_id']}, token)
   167	            if version == 1:
   168	                assert mutate('put')['state'] == 'sent'
   169	            else:
   170	                row = http(base, 'POST', '/q/peek', {'args':{'key':'a'}}, token)
   171	                assert row['data']['payload'] == 'x' * (300 * 1024)
   172	                if version == 3:
   173	                    assert row['data']['memo'] is None
   174	                    assert mutate('erase')['state'] == 'sent'
   175	                    answer = http(base, 'GET', f"/changes?since={row['seq']}&wait=0", token=token)
   176	                    answers[backend] = {k:v for k,v in answer.items() if k not in ('store_id','now')}
   177	                    print('P1-EV-1 300 KiB', backend, '/changes:', answers[backend], flush=True)
   178	            stop(child)
   179	            if backend == 'postgres':

                }
            }

                    ast::Expr::Read(r)
                    | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
                    | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => (&r.table, &r.site),

#[test]
fn current_first_worker_dependency_cannot_disappear_from_an_intent_view() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  amount: int 0..10\n  public\n  online only\nmutation create():\n  return insert docs {owner:viewer,amount:1}\n";

    app.source(&format!("{}invariant docs.read only true\n",old.replace("amount: int 0..10","amount: int 0..20")));
    fs::write(app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs"),"export {};\n").unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check {vec!["deploy","deployment","--check"]}else{vec!["deploy","deployment"]};

        assert!(text(&out).contains("authored worker bundle"),"{}",text(&out));
        assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    }

#[test]
fn scalar_key_rename_retains_an_explicit_legacy_lookup_on_default_rollback() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table items:\n  code: text <=20\n  n: int\n  unique byCode: code\n  public\n  online only\nquery legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n";
    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":"a"})]);
    let current=old.replace("  code: text <=20", "  label: text <=20 renamed from code")
        .replace("unique byCode: code","unique byCurrentCode: label").replace("n: int", "n: int ?")
        .replace("query legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n", "");
    app.source(&current);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    assert_eq!(app.json(&["sql","SELECT code FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"])["rows"][0][0],"a");
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
    assert_eq!(app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"])["rows"][0][0],"a");
}

#[test]
fn scalar_renamed_unique_keys_keep_their_identity_in_default_rollback() {
    for (kind, value) in [("text <=20", json!("alice")), ("int", json!(7))] {
        for strengthen in [false,true] {
        let app=App::new();
        let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int\n  code: {kind}\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items first 10 by byN\n");
        let old=if strengthen {old.replace("  by byN: n, id", "  by byN: n, id\n  by byCustomer: code")} else {old};
        app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        app.seed(&old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":value,"memo":null})]);
        let next=format!("table items:\n  n: int ?\n  label: {kind} renamed from code\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, label, id\n  unique byCustomer: label\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n");
        app.source(&next);
        app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
        let data=app.json(&["sql","SELECT code FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(data["rows"][0][0],value);
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
        let forward=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(forward["rows"][0][0],value);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn unchanged_quarry_cannot_roll_back_an_executable_only_policy_repair() {
    let app=App::new();
    app.source("table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  public\n  online only\ninvariant docs.read only true\n");
    let worker=app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs");fs::write(&worker,"export {}; // original\n").unwrap();
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]); // Identical executable remains a no-op.
    assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    fs::write(&worker,"export {}; // policy repair with identical Quarry\n").unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check {vec!["deploy","deployment","--check"]}else{vec!["deploy","deployment"]};
        let out=app.run(&args);assert!(!out.status.success(),"{}",text(&out));
        assert!(text(&out).contains("authored worker bundle"),"{}",text(&out));
        assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    }
}
impl App {
    fn new() -> Self {
        static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
        let p = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "sb4-roll-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        Self(p)
    }
    fn source(&self, s: &str) {
        fs::write(self.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), s).unwrap();
    }
    fn run(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        Command::new(BIN)
            .args(args)
            .current_dir(&self.0)
            .env_remove("PORT_LO")
            .env_remove("PORT_HI")
            .output()
            .unwrap()
    }
    fn ok(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        let o = self.run(args);
        assert!(o.status.success(), "{}", text(&o));
        o
    }
    fn json(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Value {
        let o = self.ok(args);
        serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}: {}", text(&o)))
    }
    fn seed(&self, source: &str, rows: Vec<Value>) {
        let b = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
        let store =
            snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&self.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite"))
                .unwrap();
        let n = store.release_value().unwrap().unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        let schema = b.schema.clone();
        let mut e = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(
            store,
            b.schema,
            b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            n,
            Some(n),
        )
        .unwrap();
        let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|mut row| {
            if row.get("photos")==Some(&json!("fixture-image")) {
                let png=b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n\x00\x00\x00\x0dIHDR\x00\x00\x00\x01\x00\x00\x00\x01";
                let asset=e.store_asset("alice",png,1000).unwrap();
                row["photos"]=json!([snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&asset.column_value())]);
            }
            (
                "items".into(),
                snapback4_core::wire::row_from_json(&schema.tables["items"], &row).unwrap(),
            )
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        e.seed(rows)
        .unwrap();
    }
    fn count(&self) -> u64 {
        self.json(&[
            "sql",
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM items",
            "--release",
            "deployment",
            "--json",
        ])["rows"][0][0]
            .as_u64()
            .unwrap()
    }
}
impl Drop for App {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}
fn text(o: &Output) -> String {
    format!(
        "{}\n{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout),
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr)
    )
}
const OLD: &str = "table items:\n  amount: int 100..50000\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable payments:\n  amount: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation pay(id: items):\n  a = items[id] ! else MISSING\n  return insert payments {amount:a.amount}\nquery read(id: items):\n  return items[id]\n";
fn newer() -> String {
    OLD.replace("amount: int 100..50000","amount: int 100..50000 ?").replace("  return insert payments","  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT 'Amount to follow must not become a zero payment.'\n  return insert payments")
}

#[test]
fn default_rollback_uses_a_compiled_guarded_view_and_exact_remains_explicit() {
    let app = App::new();
    app.source(OLD);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    let original = fs::read(app.0.join("deployment/releases/1/snapback/schema.q")).unwrap();
    let next = newer();
    app.source(&next);
    let preview = app.json(&["deploy", "deployment", "--check", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(
        preview["rollbackViewEffects"][0]["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],
        0
    );
    assert_eq!(
        fs::read_dir(app.0.join("deployment/releases"))
            .unwrap()
            .count(),
        1,
        "preview leaves no prepared source"
    );
    let deployed = app.json(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(deployed["release"], 2);
    assert!(deployed["preview"]["clientCompatibility"].is_array());
    app.seed(&next, vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
    for _ in 0..2 {
        let rollback = app.json(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "1", "--json"]);
        assert_eq!(rollback["rollbackView"]["target"], 1);
        assert!(rollback["release"].as_u64().unwrap() > 2);
        assert_eq!(app.count(), 1);
        let selected = rollback["release"].as_u64().unwrap();
        let bridge = fs::read_to_string(
            app.0
                .join(format!("deployment/releases/{selected}/snapback/schema.q")),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert!(
            bridge.contains("require (a.amount != null)")
                || bridge.contains("require a.amount != null"),
            "{bridge}"
        );
        assert!(!bridge.contains("?? 0"));
        app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "latest", "--json"]);
        assert_eq!(app.count(), 1);
    }
    let exact = app.json(&[
        "rollback",
        "deployment",
        "--to",
        "1",
        "--exact",
        "--check",
        "--json",
    ]);
    assert_eq!(exact["visibility"]["hiddenRows"], 1);
    app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "1", "--exact", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(app.count(), 0);
    app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "latest", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(app.count(), 1);
    assert_eq!(
        fs::read(app.0.join("deployment/releases/1/snapback/schema.q")).unwrap(),
        original
    );
    let releases = app.json(&["releases", "deployment"]);
    assert_eq!(releases["latest"], 2);
    assert_eq!(releases["rollbackViews"][0]["target"], 1);
    let unchanged = app.json(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(unchanged["unchanged"], true);
}

#[test]
fn hiding_acknowledges_only_null_and_keeps_widened_nonnull_values_visible() {
    let app = App::new();
    let old = "table items:\n  amount: int 0..100\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
    app.source(old);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    let next = "table items:\n  amount: int 0..1000 ?\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nrollback 1 hides items where amount = null because 'Fee to follow must not read as free.'\n";
    app.source(next);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    app.seed(
        next,
        vec![
            json!({"id":"pending","amount":null}),
            json!({"id":"quoted","amount":200}),
        ],
    );
    let preview = app.json(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "1", "--check", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"], 1);
    let out = app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "1", "--json"]);
    assert!(text(&out).contains("Fee to follow must not read as free"));
    assert_eq!(app.count(), 1);
    let values = app.json(&[
        "sql",
        "SELECT amount FROM items",
        "--release",
        "deployment",
        "--json",
    ]);
    assert_eq!(values["rows"][0][0], 200);
    app.ok(&["rollback", "deployment", "--to", "latest", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(app.count(), 2);
}

#[test]
fn deploy_verdict_leads_scoped_security_is_last_and_json_keeps_all_notes() {
    let app = App::new();
    let mut old = OLD.to_owned();
    for n in 0..8 {
        old.push_str(&format!(
            "table unchanged{n}:\n  read <- allow\n  online only\n"
        ));
    }
    fs::create_dir_all(app.0.join("versions/v1")).unwrap();
    fs::write(app.0.join("versions/v1/schema.q"), &old).unwrap();
    fs::write(
        app.0.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json"),
        r#"{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}"#,
    )
    .unwrap();
    app.source(&old);
    app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--json"]);
    let next = old
        .replace("amount: int 100..50000", "amount: int 100..50000 ?")
        .replace(
            "  return insert payments",
            "  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments",
        );
    app.source(&next);
    let out = app.ok(&["deploy", "deployment", "--schema", "snapback", "--check"]);
    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
    assert!(stdout.starts_with("deploy verdict: READY"), "{stdout}");
    assert!(
        stderr
            .lines()
            .last()
            .unwrap()
            .starts_with("deploy verdict: READY"),
        "{stderr}"
    );
    let full = app.json(&[
        "deploy",
        "deployment",
        "--schema",
        "snapback",
        "--check",
        "--json",
    ]);
    assert!(
        full["security"]["report"]["notes"]
            .as_array()
            .unwrap()
            .iter()
            .any(|n| n["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("unchanged0"))
    );
    assert!(
        !full["security"]["notice"]
            .as_str()
            .unwrap()
            .contains("unchanged0"),
        "{}",
        full["security"]["notice"]
    );
    assert!(full["clientCompatibility"].as_array().unwrap().len() > 1);
}

#[test]
fn failed_guard_transport_does_not_register_or_leave_prepared_views(){
