}

#[test]
fn text_minimum_widening_registers_a_view_that_keeps_the_current_quota() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table items:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text 4..100\n  cap 2 by owner\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,body:'kept'}\nquery all():\n  return items first 10 by .id\n";
    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
    let next=old.replace("text 4..100","text <=100").replace("public 'fixture'","read <- .owner = viewer").replace("cap 2 by owner","cap 1 by owner\n  immutable owner");
    app.source(&next);let preview=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(),1);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"short","owner":"alice","body":"x"})]);
    let selected=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--json"])["release"].as_u64().unwrap();assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    let source=fs::read_to_string(app.0.join(format!("deployment/releases/{selected}/snapback/schema.q"))).unwrap();
    let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty());
    let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&app.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(store,b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),selected,Some(selected)).unwrap();
    let hidden=engine.query("all",snapback4_core::Context::new("bob",Default::default())).unwrap();assert_eq!(snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&hidden.data),json!([]));
    let write=engine.mutate("add",None,snapback4_core::Context::new("alice",Default::default())).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(write.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}),"old quota would have admitted this second row: {write:?}");
    assert_eq!(engine.store().count("items").unwrap(),1);
}

#[test]
fn rollback_uses_active_source_context_after_a_later_branch_changed_privacy() {
    let app=App::new();let old=OLD.replacen("  amount: int 100..50000","  owner: principal\n  amount: int 100..50000",1);
    app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
    let private=old.replace("amount: int 100..50000","amount: int 100..50000 ?").replacen("  public 'fixture'","  read <- .owner = viewer",1).replace("  return insert payments","  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments");
    app.source(&private);let parent=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--json"])["release"].as_u64().unwrap();
    app.seed(&private,vec![json!({"id":"pending","owner":"alice","amount":null})]);
    let public=private.replacen("  read <- .owner = viewer","  public 'fixture'",1);app.source(&public);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to",&parent.to_string(),"--exact","--json"]);
    let selected=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--json"]);assert_eq!(selected["rollbackView"]["parent"],parent);
    let release=selected["release"].as_u64().unwrap();let source=fs::read_to_string(app.0.join(format!("deployment/releases/{release}/snapback/schema.q"))).unwrap();
    let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source)]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty());let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&app.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    let mut e=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(store,b.schema,b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),release,Some(release)).unwrap();
    let outcome=e.query("read",snapback4_core::Context::new("bob",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("pending".into()))]))).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&outcome.data),json!(null));
}


#[test]
fn invariant_apps_keep_ordinary_rename_split_and_required_field_upgrades() {
    // Small independent shapes from wave-25 002/004/020/024/036/044.
    // Real row images, an uploaded image and default rollback exercise storage,
    // rather than relying on a successful check of empty source alone.
    let base="table items:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  label: text 1..100\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nquery all():\n  return items first 10 by .id\nmutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\ninvariant items.read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let rename=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  title: text 1..100 renamed from label read <- .owner = viewer")
        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(title: text 1..100 alias label)")
        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,title}")
        +"invariant items.title read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let split=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  invitation: text 1..100\n  envelope: text 1..100")
        .replace("create(label: text 1..100)","create(invitation: text 1..100, envelope: text 1..100)")
        .replace("{owner:viewer,label}","{owner:viewer,invitation,envelope}")
        +"backfill items invitationFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {invitation:label}\nbackfill items envelopeFromLabel(label: text 1..100): {envelope:label}\ndown items oldLabel(invitation: text 1..100): {label:invitation}\ninvariant items.invitation read only .owner = viewer\n";
    let reference=base.replace("table items:","table records renamed from items:")
        .replace("return items","return records").replace("insert items","insert records")
        .replace("invariant items.","invariant records.")
        +"table attachments:\n  parent: records\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\n";
    let asset_old=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4")
        .replace("mutation create(label: text 1..100):\n  return insert items {owner:viewer,label}\n","");
    let asset_split=asset_old.replace("  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n","")
        +"table surveys:\n  parent: items\n  photos: [image <=1000] 1..4\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\nextract surveys from items via parent: {photos:photos}\n";
    let alias=reference.replace("mutation create(label:","mutation openRecord(title:")
        .replace("text 1..100):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label}","text 1..100 alias label):\n  return insert records {owner:viewer,label:title}")
        +"alias create = openRecord\n";
    let required=base.replace("  label: text 1..100","  label: text 1..100\n  priority: int 1..9 default 3");
    for(name,old,next,row)in [
        ("column rename",base.to_owned(),rename,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("split with truthful down",base.to_owned(),split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("nominal rename and child reference",base.to_owned(),reference,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("required image to child",asset_old,asset_split,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original","photos":"fixture-image"})),
        ("table operation and parameter aliases",base.to_owned(),alias,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
        ("add required with authored default",base.to_owned(),required,json!({"id":"kept","owner":"alice","label":"original"})),
    ] {
        let app=App::new();app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);app.seed(&old,vec![row]);
        app.source(&next);
        let deployed=app.run(&["deploy","deployment","--json"]);
        assert!(deployed.status.success(),"{name}: {}",text(&deployed));
        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0,"{name}: {preview}");
        let rollback=app.run(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
        assert!(rollback.status.success(),"{name}: {}",text(&rollback));
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1,"{name}");
        let rows=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(rows["rows"][0][0],"original","{name}: {rows}");
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
    }
}

#[test]
fn pre_view_release_refusal_names_exact_selection_and_registration_upgrade() {
    let app=App::new();app.source(OLD);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let next=newer();app.source(&next);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
    // A pre-0.4.39 parent has no registration manifest. Its immutable original
    // source and SQLite history are otherwise the same release contract.
    fs::remove_file(app.0.join("deployment/releases/2/rollback-views.json")).unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check{vec!["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check"]}else{vec!["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]};
        let refused=app.run(&args);assert!(!refused.status.success(),"{}",text(&refused));
        assert!(text(&refused).contains("--to 1 --exact"),"{}",text(&refused));
        assert!(text(&refused).contains("redeploy with 0.4.39 to register a view"),"{}",text(&refused));
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1,"refusal must retain the active population");
    }
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
}

#[test]
fn incomplete_current_shape_uses_a_guarded_bridge_with_current_intent() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old=OLD.replace("  amount: int 100..50000","  legacy: text <=20 ?\n  amount: int 100..50000")+"invariant items.read only true\n";
    app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let next=old.replace("  legacy: text <=20 ?\n","").replace("amount: int 100..50000","amount: int 100..50000 ?")
        .replace("  return insert payments","  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT\n  return insert payments");
    app.source(&next);let preview=app.json(&["deploy","deployment","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["rollbackViews"].as_array().unwrap().len(),1);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(&next,vec![json!({"id":"pending","amount":null})]);
    let selected=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--json"])["release"].as_u64().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    let root=app.0.join(format!("deployment/releases/{selected}/snapback"));
    let sources=fs::read_dir(root).unwrap().map(|entry|{let p=entry.unwrap().path();(p.file_name().unwrap().to_str().unwrap().to_owned(),fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap())}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let compiled=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);assert!(compiled.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",compiled.diagnostics);
    assert_eq!(compiled.invariants.len(),1);
    assert!(sources.iter().any(|(_,s)|s.contains("NO_AMOUNT")));
    let store=snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&app.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite")).unwrap();
    let mut engine=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(store,compiled.schema,compiled.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),selected,Some(selected)).unwrap();
    let out=engine.mutate("pay",None,snapback4_core::Context::new("alice",std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),snapback4_core::Value::Id("pending".into()))]))).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(out.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..}),"{out:?}");
    assert_eq!(engine.store().count("payments").unwrap(),0);
}

fn a_required_column_rename_does_not_block_an_unrelated_widening_view() {

        .replace("  amount: int 100..50000","  quotedAmount: int 100..50000 renamed from amount")
        .replace("amount:a.amount","amount:a.quotedAmount");
    app.source(&next);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);

    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    let data=app.json(&["sql","SELECT quotedAmount FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);assert_eq!(data["rows"][0][0],123);
}

    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);

    assert_eq!(plan["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
}

pub fn int_bound(end: i64, limit: i64) -> Option<i64> { (end != limit).then_some(end) }

/// Whether `value` lies inside an inclusive optional range.
pub fn int_in_range(value: i64, min: Option<i64>, max: Option<i64>) -> bool {
    min.is_none_or(|min| value >= min) && max.is_none_or(|max| value <= max)
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Index {
    pub components: Vec<IndexComponent>,
    pub unique: bool,
}

impl Index {
    pub fn of(columns: &[&str], unique: bool) -> Self {
        Self { components: columns.iter().map(|c| IndexComponent::Column((*c).to_owned())).collect(), unique }
    }

    /// Ordered key names: columns and time-function names. A `words(..)`
    /// component contributes nothing here; `word_component` names it.
    /// Use `IndexComponent::column` for actual row fields (such as rule pins).
    pub fn columns(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
    }

    pub fn is_plain(&self) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().all(|c| matches!(c, IndexComponent::Column(_)))
    }

    pub fn uses_column(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.components.iter().any(|component| match component {
            IndexComponent::Column(column) => column == name,
            IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => key.column == name,
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => fields.iter().any(|field| field == name),
            IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain) => maintain.dependencies().contains(name),
        })
    }

    /// The `words(..)` component, if any: its position among the
    /// components and the text columns it tokenizes.
    pub fn word_component(&self) -> Option<(usize, &[String])> {
        self.components.iter().enumerate().find_map(|(position, component)| match component {
            IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => Some((position, fields.as_slice())),
            IndexComponent::Column(_) | IndexComponent::Calendar(_) | IndexComponent::Contribution(_) => None,
        })
    }

    /// The columns after the first `components` components: a cursor's
    /// suffix, and the order a page continues in. Counted in components,
    /// so a scan whose prefix ends with a word skips the word too.
    pub fn suffix_after(&self, components: usize) -> Vec<String> {
        self.components.iter().skip(components).filter_map(|c| c.key_name().cloned()).collect()
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum IndexComponent {
    /// Internal selected physical source rows; an optional following expiry component
    /// orders the group by deadline (null last), then the appended source id.
    Contribution(Box<Maintain>),
    Column(String),
    Calendar(crate::maintain::CalendarKey),
    WordPrefixes { fields: Vec<String>, #[serde(default)] contract: serde_json::Value },
}

impl IndexComponent {
    pub fn key_name(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(key) => Some(&key.function), _ => self.column() }
    }
    pub fn key_type(&self, table: &Table) -> Option<ColumnType> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(_) => Some(ColumnType::Int), Self::Column(name) => table.columns.get(name).cloned(), _ => None }
    }
    pub fn value(&self, row: &Value) -> Option<Value> {
        match self { Self::Calendar(key) => Some(key.value(row)), Self::Column(name) => Some(row.field(name).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null)), _ => None }
    }
    pub fn column(&self) -> Option<&String> {
        match self {
            Self::Column(column) => Some(column),
            Self::Calendar(_) | Self::WordPrefixes { .. } | Self::Contribution(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Rules {
    pub read: RuleExpr,
    pub insert: RuleExpr,
    pub update: RuleExpr,
    pub delete: RuleExpr,
}

impl Rules {
    pub fn deny() -> Self {
        Self { read: RuleExpr::Deny, insert: RuleExpr::Deny, update: RuleExpr::Deny, delete: RuleExpr::Deny }
    }

    pub fn for_effect(&self, effect: Effect) -> &RuleExpr {
        match effect {
            Effect::Read => &self.read,
            Effect::Insert => &self.insert,
            Effect::Update => &self.update,
            Effect::Delete => &self.delete,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Effect {
    Read,
    Insert,
    Update,
    Delete,
}

impl fmt::Display for Effect {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(match self {
            Self::Read => "read",
            Self::Insert => "insert",
            Self::Update => "update",
            Self::Delete => "delete",
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RuleExpr {
    Public { reason: String },
    Allow,
    Deny,
    Eq(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    Ne(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    /// Ordered comparisons are confined to named update transitions and to
    /// insert rules over the row's own required int/time columns.
    Compare(crate::ir::BinaryOp, RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    And(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Or(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Not(Box<RuleExpr>),
    Exists {
        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
    },
    /// True only when the indexed target row was inserted earlier in this
    /// same transaction; insert rules only.
    Created {
        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
    },
}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn is_public(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. })
    }

    pub fn is_uniformly_readable(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow)
    }

    pub fn fields(&self, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {

            if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::Reference { column: field, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column: field, .. } = term {
                out.insert(field.clone());
            }
        };
        match self {
            Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow | Self::Deny => {}
            Self::Eq(left, right) | Self::Ne(left, right) | Self::Compare(_, left, right) => {

//! Table names may change; numeric row, reference and receipt lifetimes do not.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — renames are identity moves, never row copies or merges.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
pub(super) const VERSION:&str="snapback4-release-store-9";
fn refuse(s:impl Into<String>)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Structural{code:"E_ADOPT",message:s.into()}}

// Only structural table identifiers change. Literal values, column names,
// operation names and authored defaults are never table identifiers.
fn identifiers(value:&mut serde_json::Value,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>) {
    match value {
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs {identifiers(x,map)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>for (key,x) in fields {
            if key=="Literal" || key=="defaults" {continue;}
            if matches!(key.as_str(),"table"|"parent") {if let Some(name)=x.as_str().and_then(|s|map.get(s)){*x=serde_json::Value::String(name.clone());continue;}}
            identifiers(x,map);
        },_=>()
    }
}
fn mapped(name:&str,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->String {map.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
fn remap<T:serde::Serialize+serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(value:&T,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<T,RuntimeError> {
    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(value).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;identifiers(&mut json,map);
    serde_json::from_value(json).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))
}
pub(crate) fn schema(mut schema:Schema,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<Schema,RuntimeError> {
    if map.is_empty(){return Ok(schema)}
    schema.tables=std::mem::take(&mut schema.tables).into_iter().map(|(name,table)|Ok((mapped(&name,map),remap(&table,map)?))).collect::<Result<_,RuntimeError>>()?;
    for m in &mut schema.maintains {*m=remap(m,map)?;m.source=mapped(&m.source,map);m.target=mapped(&m.target,map);}
    for f in &mut schema.backfills {fill(f,map,false)?;}
    for f in &mut schema.downfills {fill(f,map,true)?;}
    for e in &mut schema.extractions {e.child=mapped(&e.child,map);}
    // Remaining table-bearing structural metadata is normalized through the
    // same typed codec; maintains' source/target are handled explicitly above.
    // These maps contain column identifiers, including arbitrary keys such as
    // "parent". Normalize their typed table slots, never their column strings.
    let mut extractions=std::mem::take(&mut schema.extractions);
    for e in &mut extractions {e.parent=mapped(&e.parent,map);}
    let columns=std::mem::take(&mut schema.asset_renames).into_iter().map(|(name,columns)|(mapped(&name,map),columns)).collect();
    schema.table_renames.clear();schema=remap(&schema,map)?;
    schema.extractions=extractions;schema.asset_renames=columns;
    Ok(schema)
}
fn fill(f:&mut crate::schema::backfill::Backfill,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>,down:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    *f=remap(f,map)?;
    f.program.name=format!("{}{}{}",if down{"down"}else{"backfill"},f.table,f.name);Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn prepare(conn:&Connection,next:&Schema,catalog:&mut BTreeMap<String,i64>)->Result<BTreeMap<String,String>,RuntimeError> {
    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_table_renames')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
    if !exists && next.table_renames.is_empty(){return Ok(BTreeMap::new())}
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _table_renames(name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,origin TEXT NOT NULL) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    let mut names:BTreeMap<String,String>=conn.prepare("SELECT name,origin FROM _table_renames")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    for (new,old) in &next.table_renames {
        if new==old || !next.tables.contains_key(new) || next.tables.contains_key(old){return Err(refuse("table rename requires a distinct predecessor-only source"))}
        let origin=names.get(old).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||old.clone());
        if names.get(new).is_some_and(|root|root!=&origin){return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} conflicts with an independent retained identity")))}
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(catalog.get(old),catalog.get(new)) {if a!=b{return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} cannot merge existing table lifetimes")))}}
        for name in [old,new] {names.insert(name.clone(),origin.clone());conn.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _table_renames VALUES(?1,?2)",params![name,origin])?;}
    }
    let mut selected=BTreeMap::<String,String>::new();
    for name in next.tables.keys() {if let Some(root)=names.get(name) {if let Some(other)=selected.insert(root.clone(),name.clone()){return Err(refuse(format!("table aliases {other} and {name} cannot be active together")))}}}
    let map:BTreeMap<String,String>=names.iter().filter_map(|(name,root)|selected.get(root).filter(|new|*new!=name).map(|new|(name.clone(),new.clone()))).collect();
    for (old,new) in &map {
        if !catalog.contains_key(old){continue;}
        if catalog.contains_key(new){return Err(refuse(format!("table rename {old} → {new} conflicts with retained table storage")))}
        let entries:Vec<_>=catalog.iter().filter(|(key,_)|*key==old || key.starts_with(&format!("{old}."))).map(|(k,v)|(k.clone(),*v)).collect();
        for (key,num) in entries {let renamed=format!("{new}{}",&key[old.len()..]);conn.execute("UPDATE _catalog SET name=?1 WHERE name=?2",params![renamed,key])?;catalog.remove(&key);catalog.insert(renamed,num);}
        conn.execute("UPDATE _assets SET home_table=?1 WHERE home_table=?2",params![new,old])?;
        for table in ["_dormant_tables","_dormant_columns","_dormant_indexes"] {
            if conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[table],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {table} SET table_name=?1 WHERE table_name=?2"),params![new,old])?;}
        }
    }
    // Retained typed definitions must use the current public table names too.
    for table in ["_dormant_tables","_dormant_columns","_dormant_narrow_rows"] {
        if !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[table],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {continue;}
        let rows:Vec<(String,String)>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT definition,definition FROM {table} GROUP BY definition"))?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        for (old,text) in rows {let mut json:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;identifiers(&mut json,&map);let new=canonical_json(&json);if old!=new {conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {table} SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2"),params![new,old])?;}}
    }
    history(conn,&map)?;
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[VERSION])?;
    Ok(map)
}
fn history(conn:&Connection,map:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    for (table,down) in [("_backfill_definitions",false),("_downfill_definitions",true)] {
        if !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[table],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {continue;}
        let rows:Vec<(String,String)>=conn.prepare(&format!("SELECT digest,definition FROM {table}"))?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        for (old,text) in rows {let mut f:crate::schema::backfill::Backfill=serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;fill(&mut f,map,down)?;let new=if down {f.down_key()}else{f.key()};if old==new{continue;}
            conn.execute(&format!("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO {table} VALUES(?1,?2)"),params![new,canonical_json(&f)])?;
            for ledger in ["_backfill_evidence","_backfill_pruned","_backfill_absence"] {conn.execute(&format!("UPDATE {ledger} SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2"),params![new,old])?;}
            conn.execute("UPDATE _backfill_absence SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2",params![format!("$native:{new}"),format!("$native:{old}")])?;
            conn.execute("UPDATE _backfill_absence SET definition=?1 WHERE definition=?2",params![format!("$authored:{new}"),format!("$authored:{old}")])?;
            for c in f.inputs.keys() {conn.execute("UPDATE _dormant_refs SET column_name=?1 WHERE column_name=?2",params![format!("$backfill:{new}:{c}"),format!("$backfill:{old}:{c}")])?;}
            conn.execute(&format!("DELETE FROM {table} WHERE digest=?1"),[old])?;
        }
    }Ok(())
}

//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
use crate::ast::*;

pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    match value {
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
        }
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
                    **read = planned;
                }
                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    match value {
                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
                                lanes.push(child);
                            }
                            continue;
                        }
                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
                    }
                }
                lanes.push(lane);
            }
            merge.lanes = lanes;
        }
        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    }
    visit(value);
}

fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
}

pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
}

pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for stmt in body {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
                expression(payload, visit);
                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
            }
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — historical lookups receive the same predicate/index planning as ordinary reads.
    for fill in doc.backfills.iter_mut().chain(&mut doc.downfills) {expression(&mut fill.value,visit);}

    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    for function in &mut doc.functions {
        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    }
    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
}

fn guard(s: &Stmt) -> Option<String> {
    match s {
        Stmt::Require {
            condition,
            code,
            message,
            ..
        } => Some(format!(
            "require {} else {}{}",
            print::expression(condition),
            code,
            message
                .as_ref()
                .map(|m| format!(" '{}'", m.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'")))
                .unwrap_or_default()
        )),
        _ => None,
    }
}
fn stmt_site(s: &Stmt) -> u32 {
    match s {
        Stmt::As { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Bind { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Expr { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Require { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Return { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Emit { site, .. }
        | Stmt::If { site, .. }
        | Stmt::For { site, .. }
        | Stmt::Break { site }
        | Stmt::Continue { site } => site.line,
    }
}

fn has_write(e: &crate::ast::Expr) -> bool {
    let mut e = e.clone();
    let mut writes = false;
    crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut e, &mut |e| {
        if matches!(e, crate::ast::Expr::Write { .. }) {
            writes = true;
        }
    });
    writes
}

// A source printer is deliberately lossy (for example it prints a merge's
// header without its lanes). Security comparison retains every AST field and
// strips only diagnostic locations. Exhaustive Expr matching makes new
// expression forms require an explicit location-normalization decision.
fn expression_signature(value:&crate::ast::Expr)->String {
    use crate::ast::{Expr,ShapeField};
    fn shape(fields:&mut [ShapeField]) {
        for field in fields {
            match field {
                ShapeField::Spread{site}|ShapeField::NamedSpread{site,..}|ShapeField::Bare{site,..}|ShapeField::Field{site,..}=>*site=crate::Site::default(),
            }
        }
    }
    fn read(r:&mut crate::ast::ReadExpr) {
        r.site=crate::Site::default();
        for k in &mut r.keys{k.site=crate::Site::default();}
        for seek in &mut r.seeks{seek.site=crate::Site::default();}
        if let Some(range)=&mut r.range{range.site=crate::Site::default();}
    }
    let mut value=value.clone();
    crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |expr| {
        match expr {
            Expr::Read(r)=>read(r),
            Expr::Exists{read:r,site}|Expr::Created{read:r,site}=>{read(r);*site=crate::Site::default();},
            Expr::Merge(m)=>{
                m.site=crate::Site::default();
                for lane in &mut m.lanes {
                    lane.site=crate::Site::default();
                    if let Some(fields)=&mut lane.project{shape(fields);}
                    if let Some(r)=&mut lane.planned{read(r);}
                    if let Some((_,r))=&mut lane.nulls{read(r);}
                }
            },
            Expr::Object{fields,site}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();for f in fields{f.site=crate::Site::default();}},
            Expr::Shape{fields,site,..}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();shape(fields);},
            Expr::Write{keys,fields,site,..}=>{*site=crate::Site::default();for k in keys{k.site=crate::Site::default();}for f in fields{f.site=crate::Site::default();}},
            Expr::Name{site,..}|Expr::Focal{site}|Expr::Literal{site,..}|Expr::Array{site,..}
            |Expr::Unary{site,..}|Expr::Binary{site,..}|Expr::Member{site,..}|Expr::Call{site,..}
            |Expr::Assert{site,..}|Expr::ShapeUse{site,..}|Expr::Walk{site,..}|Expr::If{site,..}=>*site=crate::Site::default(),
        }
    });
    format!("{value:?}")
}

fn normalized_statement(s: &Stmt) -> String {
    match s {
        Stmt::As {
            principal, body, ..
        } => format!(
            "as {} {:?}",
            expression_signature(principal),
            body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>()
        ),
        Stmt::Bind { names, value, .. } => format!("bind {names:?} {}", expression_signature(value)),
        Stmt::Expr { value, .. } => format!("expr {}", expression_signature(value)),
        Stmt::Require {
            condition,
            code,
            message,
            ..
        } => format!(
            "require {} {code:?} {message:?}",
            expression_signature(condition)
        ),
        Stmt::Return { value, .. } => format!("return {}", expression_signature(value)),
        Stmt::Emit {
            effect,
            payload,
            once,
            ..
        } => format!(
            "emit {effect} {} {:?}",
            expression_signature(payload),
            once.as_ref().map(expression_signature)
        ),
        Stmt::If {
            condition,
            then_body,
            else_body,
            ..
        } => format!(
            "if {} {:?} {:?}",
            expression_signature(condition),
            then_body
                .iter()
                .map(normalized_statement)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            else_body
                .iter()
                .map(normalized_statement)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        ),
        Stmt::For {
            name, domain, body, ..
        } => format!(
            "for {name} {} {:?}",
            expression_signature(domain),
            body.iter().map(normalized_statement).collect::<Vec<_>>()
        ),
        Stmt::Break { .. } => "break".into(),
        Stmt::Continue { .. } => "continue".into(),
    }
}

            fn asset(kind:&ast::TypeKind)->bool {match kind {ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}=>true,ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>asset(&item.kind),_=>false}}
            if (asset(&column.type_.kind)||matches!(&column.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref{..})) && column.maintained.is_none() && column.sequence.is_none() && column.written_by.is_none() {
                schema.asset_renames.entry(table.name.clone()).or_default().insert(column.name.clone(),old.clone());continue;
            }
            if matches!(&column.type_.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref{..}|ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}|ast::TypeKind::List{..})
                || column.maintained.is_some() || column.sequence.is_some() || column.written_by.is_some()
            {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("column rename on {}.{} requires an authoritative scalar without reference, asset, collection, captured or lifetime ownership",table.name,column.name),"use a bridge migration that preserves the protected field's authority and lifetime"));continue
            }
            let declaration=|input:&str,output:&str,prefix:&str|ast::BackfillDecl{
                table:table.name.clone(),name:format!("$rename:{prefix}:{old}:{}",column.name),
                args:vec![ast::ArgDecl{alias:None,default:None,deprecated:false,name:input.into(),type_:column.type_.clone(),site:column.site.clone()}],
                value:ast::Expr::Object{fields:vec![ast::ObjectField{spread:false,name:output.into(),value:Some(ast::Expr::Name{name:input.into(),site:column.site.clone()}),site:column.site.clone()}],site:column.site.clone()},site:column.site.clone()
            };
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(old,&column.name,"up"),false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(&column.name,old,"down"),true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct IndexDecl {
    pub name: String,
    /// The columns, in order, the `words(..)` component left out.
    pub columns: Vec<String>,
    pub unique: bool,
    pub refusal: Option<(String, Option<String>)>,
    /// `words(a, b)`: its position among the components (the number of
    /// columns before it) and the text columns it tokenizes. An index with
    /// one serves `matching` reads whose keys are exactly those columns,
    /// and nothing else.
    pub words: Option<(usize, Vec<String>)>,
    pub computed: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, Expr>,
    pub site: Site,
}

impl IndexDecl {
    /// Whether the index serves ordinary prefix reads and probes.
    pub fn is_plain(&self) -> bool {
        self.words.is_none() && self.computed.is_empty()
    }

    /// Whether a `matching` read with this many keys reads this index.
    pub fn searches_after(&self, keys: usize) -> bool {
        self.words.as_ref().is_some_and(|(position, _)| *position == keys)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub enum Effect {
    Read,
    Insert,
    Update,
    Delete,
}

impl Effect {
    pub fn word(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Read => "read",
            Self::Insert => "insert",
            Self::Update => "update",
            Self::Delete => "delete",

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.1 — column intent resolves to the same ordinary index as a named window.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SyncOrder {
    Index(String),
    Columns(Vec<String>),
}

impl std::fmt::Display for SyncOrder {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Index(name) => f.write_str(name),
            Self::Columns(columns) => f.write_str(&columns.iter().map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        }
    }
}

            else if self.at("set") && self.peek_at(1).value == "null" { self.take("set")?; self.take("null")?; OnDelete::SetNull }
            else { return Err(on_delete_error(&site, column)); };
        Ok((action, site))
    }

    fn parse_index(&mut self, unique: bool) -> Fallible<IndexDecl> {
        let site = self.take(if unique { "unique" } else { "by" })?.site;
        let mut explicit = None;
        if self.at_kind(Kind::Word) && self.peek_at(1).value == ":" {
            explicit = Some(self.word()?.value);
            self.take(":")?;
        }
        let mut columns = Vec::new();
        let mut words = None;
        let mut computed = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        loop {
            let component = self.word()?;
            if self.at(".") {
                return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "table index columns cannot be reference paths", "index a column of this table; use recorded paths only on a maintain").guide("indexes"));
            }
            if component.value == "words" && self.at("(") {
                // `words(body, caption)`: one entry per distinct word of the
                // named text columns, in this position.
                if words.is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, "an index has one words(..) component", "words(body)"));
                }
                self.take("(")?;
                let mut fields = vec![self.word()?.value];
                while self.maybe(",") {
                    fields.push(self.word()?.value);
                }
                self.take(")")?;
                words = Some((columns.len(), fields));
            } else if matches!(component.value.as_str(), "day" | "hour" | "bucket" | "month" | "year") && self.at("(") {
                self.position -= 1;
                let expression = self.expression(0, &[",", "else", "<newline>"]).map_err(|error| {
                    if error.code == "E_QUARRY_MAINTAIN" { crate::diag::type_error(&error.site, error.message, "day(at), hour(at, 30m), or bucket(at, 2h, 30m); declare at: time on this table").guide("indexes") }
                    else { error }
                })?;
                if computed.insert(component.value.clone(), expression).is_some() {
                    return Err(syntax_with(&component.site, format!("index repeats {}(..); each time function occurs at most once per index", component.value), "keep one day, hour and bucket component per index; declare another index for another period").guide("indexes"));
                }
                columns.push(component.value);
            } else {
                columns.push(component.value);
            }
            if !self.maybe(",") {
                break;
            }
        }
        let explicit = explicit.or_else(|| unique.then(|| {
            let descriptor = (&columns, &words, computed.iter().map(|(name, value)| (name, crate::print::expression(value))).collect::<Vec<_>>(), true);
            let hash = snapback4_core::value::digest(&descriptor);
            format!("$intent:by{}:{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>(), &hash[..16])
        }));
        let Some(name) = explicit else {
            let inferred = format!("by{}", columns.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>());
            return Err(syntax_with(&site, "index declarations require an explicit name", format!("{} {inferred}: {}", if unique { "unique" } else { "by" }, columns.join(", "))));
        };
        if let Some((position, fields)) = &words {
            if unique {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "a words(..) index cannot be unique: a row has one entry per word", format!("by {name}: {}", columns.iter().take(*position).cloned().chain(std::iter::once(format!("words({})", fields.join(", ")))).chain(columns.iter().skip(*position).cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
            }
            if *position == columns.len() {
                return Err(syntax_with(&site, "words(..) is followed by the columns a search page is ordered by, ending in id", format!("by {name}: {}words({}), at, id", columns.iter().map(|c| format!("{c}, ")).collect::<String>(), fields.join(", "))));
            }
        }
        let refusal = if unique && self.maybe("else") {
            let code = self.word()?.value;
            let message = if self.at_kind(Kind::Str) { Some(self.take_kind(Kind::Str)?.value) } else { None };
            Some((code, message))
        } else { None };
        self.newline()?;
        Ok(IndexDecl { name, columns, unique, refusal, words, computed, site })
    }

//! Computed index keys are positions, not columns in the table's row type.
use crate::ast::{Expr, ScalarType, TypeKind};
use crate::diag::{Fallible, type_error};
use snapback4_core::maintain::CalendarKey;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

// @ref LLP 3000.007#21.6 — time functions are index components at any position, sharing maintain argument rules and ordinals.
pub(super) fn key(table: &str, columns: &BTreeMap<String, ScalarType>, function: &str, expr: &Expr) -> Fallible<CalendarKey> {
    let Expr::Call { name, args, site } = expr else {
        return Err(type_error(expr.site(), "a time index component is one day, hour or bucket call, not a computed expression", "day(at), hour(at, 30m), or bucket(at, 2h, 30m); declare at: time on this table").guide("indexes"));
    };
    let form = |column: &str| if function == "bucket" { format!("bucket({column}, 2h)") } else { format!("{function}({column})") };
    if columns.contains_key(function) {
        return Err(type_error(site, format!("{table} has a column named {function}, which collides with the computed index key"), format!("rename column {function} before declaring {}", form("at"))).guide("indexes"));
    }
    let (millis, offset) = super::maintains::calendar(name, args, site).map_err(|error| type_error(&error.site, error.message, error.rewrite).guide("indexes"))?;
    let column = match &args[0] {
        Expr::Name { name, .. } => name,
        _ => return Err(type_error(args[0].site(), format!("{function} index key needs a required time column of {table}"), format!("{}  -- declare at: time on {table}", form("at"))).guide("indexes")),
    };
    if columns.get(column) != Some(&ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Time, optional: false }) {
        return Err(type_error(args[0].site(), format!("{table}.{column} must be a required time column for {function}"), format!("{column}: time  -- on {table}; use {} only in the index", form(column))).guide("indexes"));
    }
    Ok(CalendarKey { function: function.into(), column: column.clone(), millis, offset })
}

pub(super) fn calendar(name: &str, args: &[Expr], site: &Site) -> Fallible<(i64, i64)> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.040#22-builtins — UTC civil periods take no duration and no offset (time zones are deferred).
    if matches!(name, "month" | "year") {
        if args.len() != 1 { return Err(refusal(site, format!("{name} takes one time and no offset: civil periods are UTC"), format!("{name}(.at)"))); }
        return Ok((0, 0));
    }
    let base = if name == "bucket" { 2 } else { 1 };
    let rewrite = "day(time, 8h), hour(time, 30m), or bucket(time, 1d, 8h); omit the offset for UTC epoch boundaries";
    if args.len() != base && args.len() != base + 1 { return Err(refusal(site, "calendar key takes a time, a bucket duration if needed, and an optional literal offset", rewrite)); }
    let literal = |expr: &Expr| match expr {
        Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(n), site } => super::integer_literal(n, site),
        _ => Err(refusal(expr.site(), "calendar durations and offsets must be literals", rewrite)),
    };
    let millis = match name { "day" => 86_400_000, "hour" => 3_600_000, _ => literal(&args[1])? };
    if millis <= 0 { return Err(refusal(site, "bucket duration must be positive", "bucket(.at, 2m)")); }
    let offset = if args.len() > base { literal(&args[base])? } else { 0 };
    if offset < 0 || offset >= millis { return Err(refusal(args[base].site(), "calendar offset must be nonnegative and smaller than its period", rewrite)); }
    Ok((millis, offset))
}

        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => {

   554	/// Equal prefixes are adjacent in an index. Validate uniqueness and caps
   555	/// in one bounded pass per invariant, retaining one key rather than one
   556	/// allocation for every group/row in a potentially large activation.
   557	fn validate_groups(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, index: &str, prefix_len: usize, max: u64, nulls_distinct: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   558	    use crate::{error::MeterKind, meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter}, rows::word_heap as heap};
   559	    // Only encoded group keys matter. Fetching source images here would
   560	    // recreate an unmetered 500-row payload buffer after the metered rebuild.
   561	    let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT k FROM i WHERE ti = ?1 ORDER BY k")?;
   562	    let mut rows = statement.query([tx.num(&format!("{table}.{index}"))?])?;
   563	    let mut previous: Option<Vec<Value>> = None;
   564	    let mut count = 0;
   565	    let mut visited = 0;
   566	    let mut meter = Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort);
   567	    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
   568	        if visited % 256 == 0 {
   569	            meter = Meter::new(Ceilings::job(), Cut::Abort);
   570	            meter.at(&format!("adopt {table}.{index} groups"));
   571	            meter.once(crate::meter::HeapSite::SqliteColumns, 2 * tx.page_size)?;
   572	        }
   573	        visited += 1;
   574	        let scratch = meter.mark();
   575	        meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   576	        let key = row.get_ref(0)?.as_blob().map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   577	        meter.heap(heap::key_decode(key.len(), key.len()))?;
   578	        if let Some(values) = &previous {
   579	            meter.heap(heap::slots::<Value>(values.len()))?;
   580	            crate::engine::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut meter, |meter, scope| {
   581	                for value in values { value.retained_internal(scope).charge(meter, scope)?; }
   582	                Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(())
   583	            })?;
   584	        }
   585	        let mut group = crate::key::decode(key).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Store("invalid rebuilt index key".into()))?;
   586	        group.truncate(prefix_len);
   587	        if !(nulls_distinct && group.contains(&Value::Null)) {
   588	            if previous.as_ref() == Some(&group) { count += 1; } else { previous = Some(group); count = 1; }
   589	            if count > max { return Err(RuntimeError::Constraint(format!("cannot activate {table}.{index}: existing group exceeds {}", if nulls_distinct { "a unique key".to_owned() } else { format!("cap {max}") }))); }
   590	        }
   591	        meter.release_heap(scratch);
   592	    }
   593	    Ok(())
   594	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
# Time: now, durations, calendar days and the dev clock

A `time` is Unix milliseconds in UTC, and `now` is the writing host's clock.
Durations are milliseconds too; `day`, `hour`, `bucket`, `month` and `year` turn a time into a calendar number.

```quarry
use personas alice, bob

table shifts:
  owner: principal
  title: text 1..80
  startsAt: time                          -- Unix milliseconds, UTC
  endsAt: time
  createdAt: time default now             -- stamped by the writing host's clock
  immutable owner
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer
  update <- .owner = viewer
  delete <- .owner = viewer
  cap 500 by owner
  sync to .owner

view shiftsByDay: count shifts by .owner, day(.startsAt)      -- a UTC day number per group
  read <- .owner = viewer

table checkins:
  person: principal
  at: time default now
  unique person, day(at) else ALREADY_CHECKED_IN 'One check-in per day.'
  read <- .person = viewer
  insert <- .person = viewer
  sync to .person last 30 by .at

query upcoming(c: cursor ?):
  return shifts where .owner = viewer and .startsAt >= now first 20 by .startsAt after c

query today():                            -- day: 20737; midnight: 1791676800000 (2026-10-11T00:00Z)
  return { day: day(now), midnight: day(now) * 1d, shifts: shiftsByDay[viewer, day(now)] }

query pacificToday():                     -- days that start at 08:00 UTC: midnight at UTC-8, no DST
  return { day: day(now, 8h), starts: day(now, 8h) * 1d + 8h }

query thisMonth():                        -- month: 681 (2026-10), year: 2026; [start, end) as times
  m = month(now)
  return { month: m, year: year(now), start: monthStart(m), end: monthStart(m + 1) }

mutation addShift(title: text 1..80, startsAt: time, hours: int):
  require hours >= 1 and hours <= 24 else BAD_LENGTH 'A shift is 1 to 24 hours.'
  require startsAt >= now - 1d else IN_THE_PAST
  return insert shifts { owner: viewer, title, startsAt, endsAt: startsAt + hours * 1h }

mutation checkIn():
  return insert checkins { person: viewer }
```

## Values

- `time` columns and arguments hold Unix milliseconds (UTC). On the wire and in JS a time is a number:
  `Date.now()`, `new Date(row.startsAt)`. Fixture rows use the same numbers.
- `now` is the clock of the host running the operation: the server, or the device for a predicted write.
  `default now` stamps an omitted field the same way.
- Duration grammar: one non-negative integer immediately followed by `ms`, `s`, `m`, `h` or `d`; no spaces, fractions or compound `1h30m` literals. Use `1h + 30m` in Quarry.
- Duration literals: `500ms`, `30s`, `15m`, `24h`, `7d`. They are plain integers of milliseconds:
  `now + 1h`, `.endsAt - .startsAt` (elapsed ms), `hours * 1h`, `.at >= now - 7d`.
- There are no time zones and no date type. Compute local wall-clock dates, weekdays and local months in
  the client from the millisecond number; UTC months are `month(t)` below.

## Calendar numbers: day, hour, bucket

- `day(t)` is the UTC day number, `floor(t / 1d)`: 20737 is 2026-10-11, not a timestamp.
  `day(t) * 1d` is that day's midnight UTC as a time; in JS `new Date(day * 86_400_000)`.
- `hour(t)` counts **hours since the epoch**, not the hour on a clock.
  `hourOfDay(t)` returns the UTC clock hour 0..23, including before 1970.
  `bucket(t, 15m)` counts any period. At 2026-10-11 17:30 UTC,
  `hour(t)` is 497705 and `hourOfDay(t)` is 17.
- An offset moves the boundary: `day(t, 8h)` days start at 08:00 UTC (midnight at UTC-8, without DST),
  `hour(t, 30m)`, `bucket(t, 1d, 8h)`. The offset is fixed, so a zone with DST shifts by an hour.
- Use them as group keys (`view … by .owner, day(.startsAt)`), in `unique person, day(at)` (one per day),
  and in range filters (`.at >= day(now) * 1d` is "since midnight UTC").
- A `time` column accepts any integer, so storing `day(now)` there type-checks and reads as January 1970.
  Store `day(now) * 1d`, or keep the day number in an `int` column.

```quarry
query clock(t: time):
  return {ordinal: hour(t), clock: hourOfDay(t)}
```

## Months and years (UTC)

- `month(t)` counts months since 1970-01: 681 is 2026-10, -1 is 1969-12. Arithmetic is continuous:

    38	        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.")?;

    40	        let target=old.tables.get(table)?;
    41	        if target.engine_field(column) || snapback4_core::schema::timeline::is_slot(column) || old.maintain_for(table).is_some() || target.maintained.contains_key(column) {return None;}
    42	        let before=target.columns.get(column)?;
    43	        if projected(candidate,table,column,before) {return None;}
    44	
    45	        let from=candidate.tables.get(table)?;
    46	        let source_kind=from.columns.get(column).or_else(||candidate.asset_renames.get(table)?.iter().find(|(_,old)|old.as_str()==column).and_then(|(new,_)|from.columns.get(new)));
    47	        let reason=match source_kind {
    48	            None if !before.is_optional() && !matches!(target.defaults.get(column),Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(_))) =>
    49	                "without a fitting reverse projection, new rows will lack the old required field, so rollback can refuse".to_owned(),
    50	            Some(kind) if kind!=before && matches!(step.change,Change::Narrowing|Change::Destructive) =>
    51	                format!("new writes allowed by {kind} may not fit {before}, so rollback can hide rows; {}", remedy(before)),
    52	            _ => return None,
    53	        };
    54	        Some(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, {table}.{column}: {reason}; keep an expand/contract bridge with the old required fields and compatible domains; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()))
    62	    for (name,target) in old.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
    63	        let Some(from)=candidate.tables.get(name) else {continue;};
    64	        if old.maintain_for(name).is_some() {continue;}
    65	        for (column,kind) in &target.columns {
    66	            if projected(candidate,name,column,kind) || from.columns.contains_key(column) || target.engine_field(column) || snapback4_core::schema::timeline::is_slot(column) || target.maintained.contains_key(column) {continue;}
    67	            let interpretation=if kind.is_optional() {"null".to_owned()} else if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value))=target.defaults.get(column) {format!("the old default {}",snapback4_core::wire::to_json(value))} else {continue;};
    68	            let suggestions=cross_table_suggestions(candidate,old,name,column,kind,target.defaults.get(column));
    69	            risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, {name}.{column}: without a fitting reverse projection, records created while this field is absent have no retained old cell and will show {interpretation} to release {release} users; declare a reverse projection from the replacement value, or retain the field in a bridge release; {suggestions}run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    70	        }
    71	        // Registration compares constraint domains structurally in
    72	        // release_bridge::requires_view (including renamed fields). General
    73	        // foresight advice also explains constraint changes, but is not a
    74	        // second, less precise admission predicate.
    75	        if include_constraints {
    76	        for (index,definition) in &target.indexes {
    77	            if definition.unique && from.indexes.get(index)!=Some(definition) {
    78	                risks.push(format!("rollback foresight: release {release}, unique index {name}.{index}: future writes may introduce duplicate keys and refuse restoration; keep the old uniqueness in a bridge; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {release} --check --bridge <new-dir>` after writes",dir.display()));
    79	            }

**MATERIAL: changed computed unique keys can bypass rollback-view registration.**

At `66f46374e7`, consider this predecessor:

```quarry
table items:
  at: time
  other: time
  unique byDay: day(at)
  public
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation add(at: time, other: time):
  return insert items {at, other}
```

Change only `day(at)` to `day(other)`, deploy from an empty store, then insert:

| Row | at | other |
|---|---:|---:|
| A | 0 | 0 |
| B | 1 | 86400000 |

Both rows satisfy current uniqueness, but violate predecessor uniqueness: their `day(at)` values are both zero.

The source trace is:

- The [parser stores the function name `day` in `IndexDecl.columns`](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/parser.rs:754), keeping its argument separately in `computed`.
- [Uniqueness-loss detection compares only aligned `columns`](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge.rs:40). Both declarations therefore appear to enforce `["day"]`, and `requires_view` returns false.
- [Old-row foresight excludes constraint changes](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/rollback_foresight.rs:23). With unchanged columns and no other trigger, [preparation skips registration](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/rollback_views.rs:94).
- Default rollback consequently selects original source, whose [adoption validation enforces the predecessor’s computed unique key](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/adopt_refusals.rs:141) and rejects these rows.

This is ordinary declared index evolution with unchanged endpoints and bodies. The complete current closure could supply the view, but selection never reaches preparation or the new compiled-component checks.

The trigger must compare complete compiled key components, retaining the declared identity rules for plain columns.

Source-traced only; no builds, tests, edits, or review-file writes.
